- DONE "Sign-in screen fits the screen": why it scrolled: the screen was a `ScrollView` with `contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"` and no header, so iOS added the top and bottom safe-area insets as content insets on top of a `flexGrow: 1` container that already filled the frame. It always overflowed by the two insets, and `automaticallyAdjustKeyboardInsets` added the keyboard's height as more scrollable space. Fix in `src/app/sign-in.tsx`: a `KeyboardAvoidingView` (padding) around a plain `ScrollView` with `contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="never"`. The safe-area insets are now padding inside the content (the bottom inset only while the keyboard is down), with `alwaysBounceVertical={false}` and `scrollEnabled` only when the content is taller than the frame. While the keyboard is up and it overflows, it scrolls to the end so the field and button stay visible. The task's layout is followed: title and lead at the top, the shapes in a `flex: 1` space between, the field and buttons at the bottom. Board 09 puts the shapes above the title; I followed the task. The shapes are on an absolute layer, so they never add height: they shrink to fit the measured space (up to 92 pt) and drop out below half size, so the screen only scrolls when the title, field and buttons alone don't fit. Moved `useKeyboardVisible` from `components/add-bar.tsx` to `src/hooks/use-keyboard-visible.ts` so both use it. New pure `src/features/auth/sign-in-layout.ts` (`signInShapeHeight`, `signInOverflows`). Updated the DESIGN.md sign-in entry; the spec has no sign-in layout text. Tests: new `sign-in-layout.test.ts` covers full size with room to spare and at an exact fit, shrinking, rounding down, exactly the minimum, dropping out below it, zero/negative/NaN space, the row gap, and overflow with a one-point tolerance, including before the frame is measured. jest (1595), lint and tsc pass. The simulator check (SE / Pro Max, largest text size, keyboard up and down) is in manual-checks.md.
- BLOCKED before starting "Lowercase input, sentence-case display": the baseline `yarn jest` already fails (1 of 1595): `items.test.ts:752` "clearChecked and restoreItems › restore doesn't bring back an item deleted on its own before the clear" (expected 1 restored, got 2). It fails in isolation too. Cause: `deleteItem(eggs)` and `clearChecked` run within the same millisecond, so both rows get an identical `deletedAt`, and `restoreItems(ids, deletedAt)` (`src/features/lists/items.ts:468`) matches on that timestamp alone, so it brings eggs back too. This is a real (if rare) edge case in the previous task's restore matching, not just a flaky test. Possible fixes: make the clear's timestamp strictly later than any existing `deletedAt` on the list, or match on a per-clear token instead of wall-clock ms. Left unfixed per the rules; no code changed, task left unchecked.

Local migration `0011_household_aisles` (drizzle-kit couldn't answer its rename prompt without a TTY, so I generated the drops and the creates as separate steps and merged them into one migration and snapshot) first drops queued `user_category_overrides`/`categories` outbox mutations and the order mutations for custom aisles, then the order rows for custom aisles, the overrides table and the custom aisles. `categories.ts`: `categoriesQuery`/`getOrderedCategories`/`moveCategory` take the user plus one household or several; `createCategory(userId, householdId, name)` dedupes within the household; `setCategoryOverride`/`clearCategoryOverride` take the household, and clear upserts `category_id: null`; `getIngredientCategory`/`ingredientAisles` take the household. `items.ts`: `listItemsQuery(listId)` joins overrides on the item's household (the user arg is gone); `updateItem(itemId, edit)` writes the item household's override (the user arg is gone); `getItemEdit` also returns `householdId`. Sync: in `mutate.ts` both tables are household tables, and a new `unsent` spec field keeps a category's `slug`/`default_rank` and an override's `created_at` off the wire (the server rejects them); `deleteRows` is now for the order table only. In `push.ts` both go through `apply_mutations`, and the direct category insert/rename and overrides upsert are gone. In `pull.ts` both are `HOUSEHOLD_SPECS` (after households, categories before overrides), and system categories are now pulled with the catalog (`household_id is null`, `catalog:categories` cursor) so ingredient defaults still have their category. In `realtime.ts` both are on the household channel and no longer in `USER_TABLES`. `purge.ts` removes the household's overrides, custom aisles, the user's order rows for them and their queued order mutations. Call sites: item sheet (list's household), list screen, recipe screen, add bar, and the aisle order screen (system aisles plus every local household's custom ones). - BLOCKED before starting "Pending invites on the household screen" (left `- [ ]`, no code changed): the baseline `yarn jest` failed in two full runs in a row (1 failed, 1758 passed). The failure is `src/features/lists/__tests__/items.test.ts` › "clearChecked and restoreItems › restore doesn't bring back an item deleted on its own before the clear" (expected 1, received 2 at line 754). The file passes alone. Likely cause: `restoreItems` recognises the cleared items by `deletedAt` equal to the clear's timestamp (`src/features/lists/items.ts:468`). When `deleteItem(eggs)` and `clearChecked` run in the same millisecond, eggs gets the same `deletedAt` and is wrongly restored. So this is a real timing bug in the code, not only a flaky test. It needs its own fix (e.g. returning only the ids the clear actually changed, or a clear-specific marker) before the next task can start from green.

Refused calls: a non-member or an unknown household can't leave; a non-member or plain member can't remove anyone; an owner can't remove themselves or a non-member; an unauthenticated call fails; none of these change anything. A plain member leaves: outcome left with no new household; they can no longer read the household, its lists or its items, and `apply_mutations` rejects both an update and an insert; they can't leave twice; their default list is cleared; the list stays with the household. The last owner leaving promotes the earliest-joined member. An owner removes a member for whom it was the only household: that member gets a "Personal" household they own (its id is returned), with a "Groceries" list as their default, and sees only it. The last member leaving deletes the household with its lists, items and recipes, and gets a new household. `create_personal_household` places the new household after existing ones and creates a missing settings row. jest (1792, no Jest changes), lint, tsc, `supabase db reset` and `supabase test db` (454) all pass.

- DONE (2026-10-13) App: line up "N to get" with the view button (spec 4.6). Cause: `ViewMenuButton`'s outer view had `alignSelf: 'flex-start'`, which overrode the row's `alignItems: 'center'` and pinned the button to the row's top. It only lined up with the count while the row was exactly the pill's 32 pt layout height, and drifted once the count's line box grew (Dynamic Type) or the Host reported a different height. The face also used `minHeight: 44` with a fixed `-6` margin, which stops matching once the pill's label grows. The SwiftUI `Menu` adds no padding around its label (checked `MenuView.swift`), and both texts use the same `amount` variant, so neither of those was the cause. Fix in `src/features/lists/view-menu-button.tsx`: dropped `alignSelf` on the outer view (the row now centers it); the face pads the pill by a fixed `paddingVertical: inset` (6) instead of `minHeight: 44`, so the tap area is always pill + 12 ≥ 44 and the `-inset` margin gives back exactly the pill's height; the pill uses `minHeight: sizes.viewButton` so its label can't spill out at large text sizes. At the default size the pill sits where it did (row 32 pt, pill top = row top). Test: `view-menu-button.test.tsx` +1 (outer view has no `alignSelf` and margin -6; face padding 6 and no minHeight; pill minHeight 32 and no fixed height; tap area 44; layout height 32). It fails on the old code. jest (2472), lint and tsc pass. No supabase/ changes. NEEDS A MANUAL CHECK: see manual-checks.md.

New `suggestRecipeTags` task in `tasks.ts` (`AI_MODEL_TAGS`, else `AI_MODEL_RECIPE` and its fallback; `AI_MODEL_TAGS_FALLBACK`; `AI_THINKING_TAGS`; temperature 0, 15 s timeout, 400 output tokens; output cleaned with `ExtractedRecipeTagsSchema`) and `mockTagsModel` (whole-word keyword rules, never a diet). The function is in `supabase/functions/suggest-recipe-tags/` (index, handler, request, store, cli, deno.json and lock). Order: validation → `recipe_tags` (403) → `off` (503, checked before the limit so it uses no budget) → the limit (429, or 503 for the global cap) → the model (503 with Retry-After 30 s on a provider error, timeout or invalid output). It answers 200 `{ tags, promptVersion }`, caches nothing, and logs `ai_calls` with no user id; with neither model env var set it answers 500 `not_configured`. New `yarn ai:tags` script. Choices I made: the name, notes, ingredient names and notes are trimmed and *cut* (to 120, 4,000, 64 and 120) rather than refused. That follows the "truncate, never reject" rule for LLM input, and the app doesn't cap recipe or ingredient name lengths. Ingredients with an empty name are dropped before the list is cut to 80. The server accepts a recipe with no ingredients (the app won't send one). - DONE (2026-10-13) App: client and plan wiring for tag suggestions (spec 4.14). `recipe_tags` added to `FEATURES` in `src/features/plans/features.ts`. New `src/features/recipes/suggest-tags.ts`: `buildSuggestTagsRequest(recipeId)` (name, notes as stored, live ingredients in position order as `{ name: displayName, quantity, unit, note }`; null for a missing or deleted recipe) and `suggestRecipeTags(recipeId)`, which returns `{ ok: true, tags }` or `{ ok: false, error }` and never writes. Order: a missing recipe is `failed`, no live ingredients is `no-ingredients`, then `offline` (not configured, or `expo-network` says no connection; an unknown network state still calls), then no session is `offline`, then `functions.invoke('suggest-recipe-tags')`. Errors map like `import.ts` (code first, else status): 403/`not_entitled` → `not-entitled` plus `lostFeature('recipe_tags')`; 429/`rate_limited` → `rate-limited`; 503/`unavailable` → `unavailable`; FunctionsFetchError or a throw → `offline`; anything else, including a bad response body, → `failed`. Messages are in `SUGGEST_TAGS_MESSAGES` / `suggestTagsMessage`. The server answers both the per-minute and the per-day limit with the same `rate_limited` (no Retry-After), so `rate-limited` has one message, the per-minute one ("That's a lot of suggestions. Try again in a minute."). The per-day copy isn't used. Tests: new `suggest-tags.test.ts` (34): the request built from a recipe (order, units, null notes, deleted ingredients left out, deleted or unknown recipe) and the exact body sent; success never changes the recipe row or outbox; all-empty tags; bad response; no ingredients (including only deleted ones, and ahead of offline), offline, unreachable internet, no session and not configured never call the server; unknown network still calls; each code and bare status maps correctly; code beats status; network failure and a throw are offline; the exact messages; `not_entitled` drops `recipe_tags` and reloads (and drops it even when the reload fails); other errors keep it. Jest 2702 passed, 2 skipped; lint and tsc pass.

- DONE (2026-10-13) App: keep the screen on while shopping (spec 4.1). Added `expo-keep-awake` ~57.0.2 with `yarn expo install`. It's in Expo Go and has no config plugin, but an existing dev client doesn't have its native module, so rebuild the dev client once. Its `useKeepAwake` hook only follows mount, not focus, so new `src/features/lists/keep-awake.ts` has `useKeepListAwake(listId, wanted)`. It calls `activateKeepAwakeAsync`/`deactivateKeepAwake` with one tag per screen (`list:<id>:<useId>`; iOS keeps a set of tags, so screens can't release each other's lock) while wanted, the app isn't in the background (AppState) and the setting is on. The same file has `useKeepScreenOnSetting` (useSyncExternalStore) and a pure `shoppingOnList(pathname, id)`: the list counts as on screen when the pathname is `/lists`, `/lists-sheet` or `/list/<id>/...`, the same idea as the light status bar check. I used the pathname rather than navigation focus because a form sheet over the list blurs it, and the task says those sheets keep the screen on. Other tabs, pushed screens and other sheets (import-recipe, category-order) release it. `ListScreen` passes `shoppingOnList(pathname, id) && unchecked.length > 0`. Device settings gained `KEEP_SCREEN_ON_KEY` (`list.keepScreenOn`), `getKeepScreenOn` (default on; only a stored false is off), `setKeepScreenOn` and `onKeepScreenOnChange` listeners, so a switch takes effect at once on an open list. Settings > Shopping has a new **Keep screen on** row (sun.max, amber) with a `Switch` in `trailing`, the detail "While a list has items to get", and the VoiceOver label and hint the task asks for. Spec 4.1 has a new Keep screen on bullet; the spec has no separate Settings section, so the setting is described there. - App: wipe local data. New `src/features/auth/local-wipe.ts`: `wipeLocalData()` runs `WIPE_STEPS` (one per schema table, children first) in one transaction, then deletes the App Group list snapshot (`clearSiriListSnapshot`), empties the Siri and share queues (new `emptyQueues()` in `src/features/siri/app-group.ts`, `.tmp` writes too; the native log stays), and clears the plans store and cached answer (`clearFeatures`), in-memory invites, notifications and the share queue status. A DB failure throws and rolls everything back, leaving the files and stores alone. Choices beyond the task: three tables keep some rows (`KEEP_LIST`, each with a reason): `categories` keeps system aisles (custom ones go), `ingredients` keeps categorized catalog rows (pending/failed names the user typed go), and `device_settings` keeps only `KEPT_DEVICE_SETTINGS`: aisle colours, item row style, keep screen on, `importAddToList`, the catalog seed fingerprint and the two usage-backfill markers (kept so the one-time backfills don't rerun through the outbox on the next account's synced data); sync cursors/push state/HLC clock, last-opened list, view modes, collapsed sections, share attempts and `plans.features` go. `failed_imports` is wiped too. Exported `SEED_KEY` (categories.ts) and `BACKFILL_KEY`/`UNITS_BACKFILL_KEY` (usage.ts). Nothing calls it yet. Spec 4.8 has a new bullet describing the wipe. - App: drag in As added, and Reset order (spec 4.6; the long-press lines in 4.1 and 4.5 updated). New `src/features/lists/reorderable-rows.tsx` (`ReorderableRows`): each As added row sits in a Pan `activateAfterLongPress(500)` (a Pressable's long-press, so Select mode comes after the same hold). Rows can differ in height (a note, a hint), so instead of the Aisle order screen's fixed slots it measures each row and moves them with transforms in normal layout; it reuses `autoScrollStep` and the lift (1.03 scale, `shadows.lifted`, spring). Lifting without moving (≤ 8 pt) calls `onSelect`; a move then drop calls `onMove(from, to)` with a light impact once the row springs into its slot; dropping in the same place saves nothing; rows changing under a drag (a sync) end it with no move. Auto-scroll runs only while rows are hidden past that edge. The pure worklet maths is in new `row-drag.ts`: `dropSlot` (a row counts as passed once the dragged row's leading edge crosses its middle, so a tall row can still reach either end), `makeRoomShift`, `settleOffset`, `clampDrag`. `ItemRow`: new `reorderable` (the drag owns the long-press; a no-op `onLongPress` so a long hold never ends in an open) and `moves` (Move up / Move down, after the existing actions). List screen: `applyManualOrder` after `orderByNewest`, with state mirroring `getManualOrder`. The list ref is now `useAnimatedRef` on the same plain FlatList (scrollToOffset/scrollToIndex unchanged), and `scrollEnabled` is off while dragging. Drag and the Move actions work only in As added, outside Select mode and add mode, and not on a checked row lingering in place. A drop or Move saves via `saveManualOrder` (given all items, so checked entries keep their places); Move announces e.g. "Milk, 2 of 3" (`aisleRowLabel`). `ViewMenuButton` takes `onResetOrder`, which adds a second Section with Reset order (`arrow.uturn.backward`); it's passed only in As added with a saved order. It clears with no confirm and shows the toast "Order reset" with Undo (which writes the previous entries back). `manualOrderKey` isn't in `KEPT_DEVICE_SETTINGS`, so the wipe clears it. Tests: new `row-drag.test.ts` (15) and `reorderable-rows.test.tsx` (9: hold time and lift haptic, still lift selects, drop moves once with impact, up/down past uneven rows and clamped ends, same place saves nothing, release without lift, rows changing mid-drag, disabled row, second drag). list-screen.test +9 (`dragging in As added`: order from a saved setting with a new add on top and garbage ignored, drop saves and survives a view switch and remount, long-press without moving enters Select mode, no drag in add mode / By aisle / By recipe, lingering checked row, VoiceOver Move up/down at the ends with announcements, Reset order and Undo, toast gone after 5 s, a checked item returns to its place). item-row.test +2. local-wipe.test's seed and "gone" list now include the manual order key. - Spanish amounts in the quantity parser (2026-10-14, spec 4.15): `src/shared/quantity-parser.ts` now reads Spanish number words (`un`/`una`/`uno` … `veinte`, `dieciséis` with or without the accent, `medio`/`media` = 0.5). It maps the spec's Spanish unit spellings to existing units (`taza(s)`, `cucharada(s)`/`cda(s)`, `cucharadita(s)`/`cdta(s)`, `gramo(s)`, `kilogramo(s)`, `litro(s)`, `mililitro(s)`, `libra(s)`, `onza(s)`, `galón`/`galones`, `lata(s)`, `frasco(s)`, `bolsa(s)`, `caja(s)`, `manojo(s)`, `diente(s)`, `pizca(s)`, `docena(s)`; `kilo(s)` and `gr` were already there). `pieza(s)`/`pza(s)` is a plain count, and one `de` after a unit or count word is dropped, as `of` is. Lookups ignore accents and case. `medio`/`media` follow `half`'s guard (an amount only before a unit or article), so `media crema` and `medio pollo` stay names. The other Spanish number words count like `two` (`dos aguacates` → 2 `aguacates`). FOR THE REVIEWER: I read the task's "a name that merely starts with a number word and no unit stays a name" as the `half and half` guard applied to `medio`/`media`, not to every number word, because English already parses `two avocados` → 2. So `tres leches` parses as 3 `leches`. Tests: a new "Spanish" block in quantity-parser.test.ts. It has a 44-row table with every example from the task plus every unit spelling, accents, case, fractions, glued `500gr`, count words, mixed English and Spanish, and `de` dropped only after a unit or count word and only once. It also covers names kept as names (`media crema`, `medio pollo`, `medio`, `una`), a unit or count word with nothing after it, unknown words (`paquetes`, `pan de muerto`) and `parseAmount` with Spanish. No existing tests changed. There is no `siri-add` Edge Function in this repo yet (Siri adds are drained in the app by `src/features/siri/drain.ts`). Handler: saves `lang` with the category (`other` → null, never over a known one); then per answer `cleanTranslation` (trimmed, runs of spaces collapsed, only letters/spaces/hyphens/apostrophes, ≤ 64 characters before and after normalizing, key neither empty nor the name's) and `saveTwin`: inserts the twin (`insertTwins`: categorized, the name's category/unit/grocery flag, the other `lang`, `categorized_by` the model, `household_count` 0, on conflict do nothing) and, for an exact equivalent, links non-English → English with `linked_by` the model, unless either side has a link, the non-English row is already a target, or an existing twin's `lang` is the wrong one; the store's `link` writes only where `same_as is null` and turns the one-level trigger's check_violation into "not linked". Results gain `lang`, `same_as`, `linked_by`; the response gains `twins` (rows created or linked that weren't input names, with `display_name`). Taps are kept in the screen's state. A tap places the recipe, or takes out a slot placed in this visit and shows what was there before. The toast reads "Planned for Thu dinner" or "Replaced Lemon ricotta pasta on Sat dinner", and its Undo repeats the tap. The CTA is "Done" / "Done · N planned"; Done calls `applyPlanChanges` once, and Cancel or swiping away writes nothing. - Shop this week (2026-10-15, spec 4.16, 4.4, 4.5, board 3 G): new `src/features/meal-plan/shop.ts`. `shopDays` gives today to Saturday this week, every day of a future week, and none of a past one. `shopTitle` gives "Shop the rest of the week" / "Shop the week of Oct 18". `shopEntries` takes each slot's shown entry that's a recipe, in day then meal order. `recipesOnList` finds recipes with an amount on an open item of the list, counted as the "On Groceries" badge counts them. `shopLines` builds the ticked entries' ingredients at 1× through `buildRecipeAdds`, merged by their own ingredient key; each entry counts once, so a recipe planned twice adds twice, and staples are flagged. The add goes through `addRecipeToList`/`addItemsBulk` as one undoable add, with a contribution per recipe. The toast reads "Added 3 recipes · 18 items" with Undo (`undoRecipeAdd`) and Open list; `openShopSheet`/`showShopToast` hand it back to the week that opened the sheet. New route `src/app/plan-shop.tsx` (a formSheet in the root layout; params householdId, start) shows the title, then the household's open lists with the current one first (picking a list re-reads "already on" and resets the ticks). Next come the entries (tick, tile, name, "Thursday dinner · already on Groceries"), a footnote, **Review N items** and **Add N to Groceries**. Review is in the same sheet: Add to a list's "Untick what you have" checklist of the merged lines, staples unticked, with Back. My calls: N and the toast's item count are list items (merged lines / distinct items touched), not raw amounts. Review merges by each ingredient's own key, so a pepino/cucumber pair shows as two lines but still lands as one item when the household combines languages. "Already on" ignores checked items. `WeekPlan` enables the cart only when `shopEntries` over `shopDays` has entries; `WeekToolbar`'s round button gained a disabled state. Add to a list's `SheetLayout`, `Label` and `IngredientChoice` moved unchanged to `src/features/recipes/add-sheet.tsx` (`SheetLabel`, plus `toggled`) so Review reuses them. `.expo/types/router.d.ts` (generated, ignored) was hand-edited for `/plan-shop`. en/es strings. Tests: new `shop.test.tsx` (24): days for this, future and past weeks, including Sunday and Saturday; titles in en/es; entries from today on, with notes, earlier days and deleted recipes left out; the sheet's title, list order (current first), other households' and archived lists left out, entry labels and ticks; the already-on-list default, a checked item not counting, switching lists; Add without staples, a recipe planned twice, merging across recipes and with an existing item, only ticked entries, the toast; Undo restores the list exactly; Open list; CTAs disabled with nothing ticked and with only staples; Review's lines, amounts and staple ticks, and adding a ticked staple; Back/Cancel; the cart opening `/plan-shop` for this and next week, and disabled for a past week and when the days it covers have no recipes. - The eval first: step ingredients (2026-10-16, spec 4.5, 6; docs/spikes.md §5): **done, go.** New AI task `matchRecipeSteps` in `_shared/ai/tasks.ts` (`AI_MODEL_STEPS`, else `AI_MODEL_RECIPE`, fallbacks likewise; `AI_THINKING_STEPS`; 20 s timeout; `stepsMaxOutputTokens` 200 + 80 per step, capped at 6,000) with `MatchRecipeStepsOutputSchema` and `cleanStepMatch` (steps that don't line up → null, which `matchRecipeSteps` throws as `steps_mismatch`; unknown or repeated ingredients dropped; a share is kept only when positive and below the ingredient's quantity, rounded to 3 decimals; uses in recipe order). Prompt `prompts/match-recipe-steps.v1.ts`, its rules in `prompts/step-match-rules.ts` for the later import task. Mock `mockStepsModel` (an ingredient's last name word in the step text). `supabase/functions/match-recipe-steps/`: `match.ts` (`matchRecipeSteps`, `StepMatchRecipeSchema`, `StepMatchError`; no index.ts yet, the Edge Function is a later task), `cli.ts` (`yarn ai:steps <url>`, `--file`, or `--step`/`--ingredient`; a link or file goes through `importRecipe` with categorize on the mock), `eval/eval.ts` (`yarn ai:eval:steps`, `--only`, `--price-in`/`--price-out`) and `eval/recipes.json` (16 recipes: 13 import-eval JSON-LD pages with the import model's ingredients frozen in, plus typed tacos with split limes, Spanish sopa de fideo, and an injection step; 100 steps, 220 needed pairs, hand-labelled). Results: gemini-3.8-flash 100% precision, 100% recall, all shares right, p50 2.5 s / p95 8.0 s; gemini-3.5-flash-lite 99.1% / 100%, p50 1.2 s / p95 2.0 s; ~1,000 in / 200 out tokens per recipe. Decision: keep `AI_MODEL_STEPS` unset (follows the recipe model). Docs: `docs/llm-calls.md`, `docs/spikes.md` §5, `.env.example`. Tests: 27 new Deno tests in `match-recipe-steps` pass; suggest-recipe-tags (58), import-recipe (119) and categorize-ingredients (290) still pass; `yarn tsc` and `yarn expo lint` clean.

New `src/features/lists/last-seen.ts`: `getListSeenAt`, `markListSeen` (skips a list the device no longer has, so a screen closing after a purge leaves nothing behind), and `useListSeenMarker(listId, onScreen)`, wired into `ListScreen` with `shoppingOnList` (the Keep screen on rule). It marks a list shown for the first time at once, and marks it again whenever it stops being on screen in the foreground: another tab, a pushed screen or another list's sheet, the app backgrounded (not `inactive`), or the screen unmounting (switching lists, deleted, archived). `useForeground` is pulled out of `keep-awake.ts` for both hooks. `listNewsSince(listId, since, me = account)` is a re-runnable PromiseLike for `useLiveQueryOn(…, LIST_NEWS_QUERY_TABLES)`: live unchecked items with a live contribution from another member, created after `since - 2 min` (`SEEN_CLOCK_SKEW_MS`), grouped by `groupListNews` per member (each item once per member, dated by that member's latest add, newest first; members with the most items first, then the most recent). An item two members both added to is listed under both, so the card task should dedupe its total. `purgeHousehold` clears the markers. - A row that arrives while you look (2026-10-16, spec 4.1): new `src/features/lists/arrivals.ts`. `useListArrivals(listId, items, onScreen, me)` keeps a visit per list (on screen by the Keep screen on rule, in the foreground). It starts from `readListContents`, a synchronous read of the list's live contribution ids and items to get, so a live query that loads late can't look like arrivals. It's worked out during render as the live query's rows change, so a new row mounts already knowing it arrived. `detectArrivals` picks items to get with a contribution not seen before, from another member (not me on any device, not a null `added_by`), made on the server after the visit started less `SEEN_CLOCK_SKEW_MS`, so pull catch-up doesn't count. `grow` is set when the item wasn't already to get (new, or a checked item re-added). It returns `cues` (flash and grow, for the visit), `tags` (cleared by `clearTags` on the FlatList's `onScrollBeginDrag`), `unseen` (for the pill, `markSeen`) and the latest batch for VoiceOver. Leaving the list clears all of it. `ItemContribution` now carries `addedBy` (`listItemsQuery` selects it). `ItemRow` takes `arrival` ({ key, grow }), `tag`, `arrivalRef` and `onArrivalLayout`. `arrival` uses `growIn`, a custom Reanimated entering in `use-motion.ts` that opens the height over `Motion.duration` and is skipped under Reduce Motion, and `ArrivalFlash`, a `pendingSoft` overlay that fades out over the new `Motion.flashMs` token and restarts on a new contribution key. `tag` is a `surface` pill before the amount: a 20 pt avatar (new `Sizes.tagAvatar`) in `memberHue` and the short name in `caption` in the hue's `strong`; the row label adds "added by Alex". `ListScreen` wraps the FlatList in a measured viewport view. Arrived rows are measured with `measureInWindow` after each arrival, layout and scroll (`inSight`: the row's middle is inside the list's frame; a row that isn't drawn, e.g. in a folded section, is out of sight). Rows out of sight show `ArrivalPill` 8 pt above the add bar ("2 new from Alex" / "3 new", `arrivalPillText`). It scrolls to the first in list order: centred, or opening the section and using `scrollToIndex` when the row isn't drawn. `useArrivalAnnouncements` announces at most once per `ANNOUNCE_EVERY_MS` (5 s): the first at once, then the rest grouped (`arrivalAnnouncement`: "Alex added limes", "Alex added 3 items", a sentence per member). What's waiting is dropped on leaving. No haptic. Strings are under `lists.arrivals` in en/es. The existing add-merge highlight (aisle `soft` while its toast shows) is unchanged; the arrival flash is its own fade with the new token. Spec 4.1 has a new "A row that arrives while you look" bullet; DESIGN.md has an "Arrival cue" entry. Tests: new `arrivals.test.ts` (20: another member's add, a Combine, my adds, null `added_by`, catch-up before the visit and the skew edge, a checked item, a checked item re-added grows, several amounts at once, signed out; `readListContents`; `inSight` edges; the pill for one member, several, none, Someone, Spanish; announcement wording, grouping, dedupe, several members, Spanish). `item-row.test.tsx` "an arrival" (8: grow and clip, the Reduce Motion path, a Combine doesn't grow, the flash colour and `flashMs` fade, a re-flash on a new key, the tag's look, order and label, no arrival, the ref and layout callbacks). Its Reanimated mock gained `useSharedValue`, `useAnimatedStyle`, `withTiming` and a Reduce Motion switch; no existing test changed. `list-screen.test.tsx` "a row that arrives while you look" (14: another member's add, a Combine, my adds, first load and pull catch-up, every view mode, the tag cleared by scrolling, leaving and the list's own sheets, background, a checked-off arrival, the off-screen pill counting, scrolling and clearing, a row landing in sight, a folded section opened by the pill, announcement throttle and grouping with no haptic, announcements dropped on leaving). New migration `20261027153000_list_presence.sql`: `private.can_use_list_topic(topic)` (security definer: `list:<id>` for a list in one of the caller's households, compared as text) and two policies on `realtime.messages` for `authenticated`, select (join and receive) and insert (track), both only for `extension = 'presence'`. - Drag to reorder in As added moves rows live (2026-10-16, spec 4.6): the cause was each row's `onLayout` doing `boxes.set({ ...boxes.get(), [id]: box })` on the JS thread. In Reanimated 4.5 a JS-thread `set` is only scheduled onto the UI thread while `get` reads the UI value now, so rows laying out together overwrote one another and only the last row's box survived. The rest counted as zero height, so nothing made room and the drop slots were wrong. Fix: `boxes.modify(all => withBox(all, id, box))`, which runs in order on the UI thread. Also pulled the drag reaction's math into a pure `dragTo(rows, from, translation, scrolled)` in `row-drag.ts`, placed below `clampDrag` because the worklets plugin turns worklet functions into consts, which aren't hoisted. Tests: `row-drag.test.ts` adds `dragTo` (down and up past several rows including a taller one, across an auto-scroll both ways, clamped at the ends, no boxes) and `withBox`. `reorderable-rows.test.tsx`'s Reanimated mock now defers JS-thread `set`/`modify` until the UI thread runs, as on device (an existing test file's mock changed, no assertions weakened), and adds two tests that the other rows make room live, down and up. Those two tests and two existing drop tests fail with the old `onLayout`. NEEDS A MANUAL CHECK (in manual-checks.md).

New `mergeSuggestions(history, catalog, query, limit)` orders both sources the same way, then history (in `rankHistory` order) before the catalog, then alphabetical; dedupe by key (history wins) and the limit unchanged. `searchRecentItems` also pre-filters on the key being the typed name's. New `catalogEntry(name)` in `categories.ts` looks up the catalog grocery row for the typed name; `getSuggestions` always includes it, while with 2+ history matches the rest of the catalog is still left out. JSDoc on `rankHistory`, `getSuggestions`, `searchRecentItems` and `suggestionChips` updated; spec 4.2 **Ranking** rewritten and **History first** notes the exact-match exception. Existing tests changed because the order changed: suggestion-rank.test.ts (the two frecency tests use equal-length names; "prefix ahead of word" and "higher-scoring word beats prefix" became "at equal length prefix beats a higher-scoring word match" and "a shorter word match beats a longer prefix match"), items.test.ts "applies the limit after ranking" (now chili, chive), suggestions.test.ts (one-history-match/dedupe, hidden rows, applySuggestion, hide-drops-below-2 use queries/expectations for the new order), categories.test.ts autocomplete (adds `chickens`). It has a row per meal, at least planCell (64), with hairlines between. A recipe row has a sizes.planOpenPhoto (76) 4:3 photo, the meal in its hue's strong, the name in headline (2 lines) and the time from recipeTimeLabel in footnote inkSecondary. A note row shows the note and "Note". An empty row shows a dashed photo-sized box with a + and "Nothing planned", with no outline or + on past days, as in the compact cells. Rows keep the slot testIDs and VoiceOver labels and call onSlot. Board D also shows "Tap to plan" under an empty row, but the task names only "Nothing planned", so that's all it shows. WeekPlan holds the open day (today to begin with), so it's kept across weeks and the view toggle while the screen is mounted, and a week without that day shows every day closed. In expand style each day row is an Animated.View with a LinearTransition over Motion.duration, and none under Reduce Motion. That lives in its own component, so the compact and photo grids and the plan modal never call reanimated. The plan modal passes no onToggleDay, so it shows compact rows only. Strings are in en/es, and spec 4.16 describes Expand. I changed one existing test: week-plan.test.tsx's "compact and expand styles" test.each now covers compact only (plus a check that its labels aren't buttons), since expand no longer draws as compact. The reanimated mock there gained LinearTransition and useReducedMotion. Tests added: week-plan.test.tsx "the grid in the expand style" (photo strips with and without a photo; notes and empty cells at 104; today open by default; the label, its state and tap target; opening another day closes the first, and a second tap closes it; kept across weeks and views; no open day in a week without today; the card's recipe, note and empty rows; "1 hr 30 min" and no time line; a past open day; the layout transition and Reduce Motion; the card's width at large text; row taps calling onSlot with a recipe, a note and null; label taps calling onToggleDay; no opening without onToggleDay). plan-recipe.test.tsx "in the expand grid style" (no day opens, labels aren't buttons, strips, a tap still places). week-view.test.ts: dayToggleLabel in English and Spanish. jest 4444 passed, lint and tsc clean. - Step cards (2026-10-16, spec 4.5, board B): cook mode's Method card is now one Card per step, spacing.md apart in a column. There's no Method header and no "Up next" caption, since board B has neither, so the `recipes.cook.upNext` string is gone. StepCard has three states. Done: a primarySoft badge with a primary check, the text in callout inkSecondary, nothing else. Current (the first unticked step): a 2 pt primary border (new `sizes.cookCurrentBorder`), a primary badge with an onPrimary number, the text in the new `cookStepCurrent` typography (cookStep at 600), its ingredient rows in the existing inset (now on `background`), then a full-width PrimaryButton "Mark step done", read by VoiceOver as "Mark step 3 done". Upcoming: the outlined controlBorder badge, the text in body and, with a match, a footnote inkSecondary line. The line is built by the new `stepUseText` in cook-match.ts and joined with " · ": "2 tbsp butter", "1 of 3 limes", "2 lb chicken thighs, part", "spaghetti", all scaled. VoiceOver skips the line because the step's label already names those ingredients. Mark step done ticks the step and its uses and sets a pending scroll target. When that card next lays out in its new place, the ScrollView scrolls it to spacing.md below the top, with animated false under Reduce Motion (reanimated's useReducedMotion). It waits for layout because the card above shrinks once it's done, so positions measured before the tick are wrong. The source credit sits under the last card through a new `underCard` option on RecipeCredit (no hairline, lined up with the cards' text). New en/es strings: markDone, markDoneLabel, useText, useTextPart. DESIGN.md has the new cook screen paragraph, a cookStepCurrent row and cookCurrentBorder. Spec 4.5 has a new "Cook mode's step cards" paragraph naming board B, and an updated step-match paragraph. I changed existing tests in recipe-cook.test.tsx because the task changes what they check. The old "ringed circle"/Method header, "tapping a step" and "Up next tints" tests are replaced by tests for the card column and the three states. The step-match tests now expect rows only on the current card, with step 3's rows checked after ticking steps 1 and 2. "Also needed … after Method" now compares with the last step's text. The reanimated mock's useReducedMotion now reads a mockReduceMotion flag. Tests added: one card per step, 12 pt apart, with no Method header; the upcoming state; the done state; the current state with Mark step done and its VoiceOver label; Mark step done ticking through to "For 4 · 3 of 3 steps done". The scroll: it happens only after Mark step done, only for the new current card and only once, at y = column + card − 12, with animated false under Reduce Motion, and not on a plain tap or the last step. Also: the current card's rows as steps are ticked; the upcoming line's text, style, indent and VoiceOver hiding; rows and line scaled to 8 servings; Mark step done ticking ingredients; the no-match layout; the credit under the last card; stepUseText unit cases. jest 4452 passed, lint and tsc clean. - Ingredient pills on the current step (2026-10-16, spec 4.5, board B): in cook.tsx the current card's inset rows are replaced by a new IngredientPill. Each is a surfaceMuted pill with radius full, at least 44 pt tall (minTapTarget), with a 22 pt CheckCircle (cookIngredientCheck) and the use in callout, built by the existing stepUseText ("2 tbsp butter", "1 of 3 limes", "2 lb chicken thighs, part", scaled). Ticked, the check fills and the text goes inkSecondary. The pills sit in a flexWrap row, 8 pt apart, indented to the step's text. From a fontScale of 2 (a local PILLS_STACK_FONT_SCALE, AX3 and up, the same threshold as the Recipes grid's one column) they go in a column and stretch one per line. VoiceOver labels and checkbox states are unchanged, and so is the tick model in cook-match.ts. The `compact`, `first` and `label` props are gone from IngredientRow, since nothing else used them. DESIGN.md's cook screen paragraph and cookIngredientCheck, and spec 4.5's step-match and step-card paragraphs, now describe pills. I changed one existing test because the task changes what it checks: in recipe-cook.test.tsx "the current step lists its ingredients…", a pill's texts are now one phrase (['1 of 3 limes'], ['2 lb chicken thighs, part']) instead of the name and the amount. recipe-cook.test.tsx now mocks useWindowDimensions with fontScale 1, because RN's jest setup reports 2. Tests added in "the current step's ingredient pills": pill text and VoiceOver labels/states for plain, share and part uses, then scaled to 8 servings (4 tbsp shows as ¼ cup); the pill's style (surfaceMuted, full radius, 44 pt, 22 pt check, ink text) and the indented wrapping row; tapping a pill ticks that use and updates All ingredients ("4 · 1 ticked"), a shared lime's one use leaves its row unticked, and pills don't tick the step; ticking from All ingredients ticks the pill; at fontScale 2.35 the pills go one per line (a column, stretched); at 1.95 they still wrap. jest 4458 passed, lint and tsc clean.

A photo mock returns a fixed "Mock photo recipe", or not a recipe when the first image is under 1 KB. New Edge Function `import-recipe-photo`. Its handler runs plan → kill switch → limits → one call → `cleanExtractedRecipe` → the link import's own categorize step and response, now `importResponse()` in import-recipe/handler.ts. It has a store, index and request layer (400/403/405/413/422/429/500 not_configured/503). Migration `20261030120000_recipe_photo_import.sql` adds `check_recipe_photo_limit` (5 a minute, 30 a day) and a retention cron job. `38_recipe_photo_import_limit.test.sql` has 25 checks. The migration and that test have NOT BEEN RUN. CLI `import-recipe-photo/cli.ts` and `yarn ai:recipe-photo`. The script needs no --allow-write: sips writes into $TMPDIR, and nothing is deleted. The trace got a `redact` option so `--raw` shows image data as `<N bytes base64>`. import-recipe's `formatRecipe` and `formatCallStats` take an optional source or task label. Spec: the task says "write spec 6.9", but 6.9 is already Page photos, so I wrote it as §6.10 Photo import. I also updated 4.12's `recipe_import_photo` line, 6.4's list of AI tasks and 6.6's Prompt bullet, plus docs/llm-calls.md and .env.example. Tests added in Deno: import-recipe-photo/handler.test.ts (21): the mock's two paths; entitlement before the kill switch and limits; the kill switch before the limits for each env combination; the user limit and global cap; image parts in order; ai_calls holding no image bytes; cleaning, with image_url ignored; provider, invalid-output and unexpected errors; categorize's own limit; no caching. request.test.ts (12): image count; size at and over 2 MB; media types; bad base64; bad JSON; 413 by header and as read; 405; not_configured; status and Retry-After per failure; dailyPhotoCap; 401 from the deployed function. prompt.test.ts (9): the shared rules; the photo rules; the content and recorded prompt; call limits; model, fallback and thinking env. cli.test.ts (17): parseArgs; mediaTypeFor; toBase64; redactBase64; runs through the mocks, including --json and --quiet; not a recipe; off; HEIC via sips; no sips; resizing over 2 MB; still over after resizing, or sips failing; usage errors; the env fallback and restore. Plus the prompt-v4 byte-for-byte pin. Tests added in jest: src/shared/__tests__/recipe-photo-import.test.ts (8); a nullable-sourceUrl case in recipe-import.test.ts; two import.test.ts cases (a URL-less response makes a new recipe without matching existing ones; no name and no URL is "Recipe"). I changed one existing test for types only: ensure-step-match.test.ts now passes `IMPORTED.sourceUrl!` in 3 places, because the typed fixture's `sourceUrl` is now `string | null`. Deno: import-recipe-photo 59, import-recipe 153, categorize 290, suggest-recipe-tags 58, match-recipe-steps 60, recipe-photos 14, all passing. jest 4469 passed; lint and tsc clean. - Photo import: in the app (2026-10-17, spec 4.5, 4.10). The Add recipe sheet's link card has a new From photo… row (FromPhotoRow in add-recipe.tsx): a hairline above, a primarySoft iconWell with a camera, "From photo…" over "A cookbook page or a screenshot", an inkTertiary chevron, at least minTapTarget tall, VoiceOver "From photo, button". It shows only with recipe_import_photo, and since it sits inside the link group the plan also needs recipe_import, which plus has. It opens an ActionSheetIOS with Take photo / Choose from library / Cancel. New src/features/recipes/photo-import.ts has IMPORT_PHOTO (2000, 0.8) and pickImportPhotos. The library allows multiple selection with a selectionLimit of 3, keeps the order picked and doesn't crop; the camera takes one shot after asking for access. It also has collectImportPhotos (after each camera shot it asks "Is there another page?" with Add another page / Use photos, up to 3; a refusal shows the existing camera-denied alert) and photosParam/photosFromParam. The pages go to /import-recipe as a `photos` JSON param. The sheet then shows "Add recipe from photo", "Pages" and 3:4 thumbnails (new sizes.importPageThumb 72, added to DESIGN.md) instead of the link field, with the same household, switch and list choices. import.ts has a new importRecipeFromPhotos. It saves through the same path as a link import (tags, time, steps, step match, list adds, toast, Undo). errorResult now takes the function name and the feature that not_entitled drops (recipe_import_photo for photos). importErrorMessage takes 'link' | 'photo' ("That photo doesn't look like a recipe", "Photo import isn't in your plan"). - Photo credit on recipes (2026-10-17, spec 4.5, 5). New migration 20261031120000_recipe_photo_credit.sql adds `recipes.photo_credit jsonb` with the check `recipes_photo_credit_shape`. The check is written inline with cases, no function, and requires exactly provider 'pexels', author 1–200 chars, and authorUrl/pageUrl that are https:// and ≤ 2048 chars, only when photo_path is set. apply_mutations needed no change. Local side: new type `PhotoCredit` in src/shared/photo-credit.ts, `photoCredit` (json text) in src/db/schema.ts, and SQLite migration 0022_recipe_photo_credit (generated by drizzle-kit: journal, snapshot, migrations.js). Push and pull are generic, so no mapping code changed. `RecipePhotoFields` now has `photoCredit`, plus a new `RecipePhotoSourceFields` where the credit is optional, so tiles that show no credit don't pass one. `useRecipePhoto` returns `credit`, which is the own photo's credit, else null. pagePhoto and photoAfterRemove clear the credit. changedFields sends `photoCredit` whenever `photoPath` changes, even when the credit is null both times, and also when only the credit differs. My reason for always sending it with the path: the two then share one HLC and win or lose a merge together, so a merge can't leave a credit without its photo_path, which the server would refuse. Undo restores all three fields. uploadRecipePhoto(recipeId, localUri, credit = null) sets the credit together with photo_path; PhotoUpload carries `credit`, so Try again re-sends it. Spec 4.5 and the 5 recipes table are updated. Tests: new supabase/tests/39_recipe_photo_credit.test.sql (24) covers a valid credit, boundary lengths, clearing, and refusals for a bad provider, a missing key, an extra key, http:// author or page URLs, an empty or 201-char author, a URL over 2048 chars, a non-string author, a non-object credit, a credit with no photo_path, and clearing photo_path while the credit stays. It also covers apply_mutations applying, rejecting and clearing, with the credit sharing the photo's clock. Jest: recipe-photos.test.ts (pagePhoto and Remove clear the credit, Undo puts it back, a new path always carries its credit, a credit-only change, a stock-over-stock Undo), photo-upload.test.ts (an upload with a credit, a library upload clearing it, Undo, a failed upload's retry keeping the credit), recipe-photo-source.test.tsx (useRecipePhoto's credit), pull.test.ts (a credit pulled, the push→pull round trip, a newer remote clear winning, a server without the column), and recipe-photo-sheet.test.tsx (a page photo over a stock photo, then Undo). Existing tests changed because the behaviour changed: mutate.test.ts and write-paths.test.ts expect `photo_credit: null` in a recipe insert, and four recipe-photo-sheet.test.tsx assertions expect `photo_credit: null` sent alongside every `photo_path` change.
- photo-search Edge Function (2026-10-17, spec 6.11). New shared src/shared/schemas/photo-search.ts: PhotoSearchRequestSchema ({ query trimmed 1–100, page int 1–10 default 1 }), PhotoSearchResultSchema ({ id, thumb, full, width, height, author 1–200, authorUrl, pageUrl, alt | null }, every URL https:// and ≤ 2048, the limits recipes.photo_credit takes), PhotoSearchResponseSchema ({ photos ≤ 20, hasMore }), the error codes and photoSearchKey (lowercased, whitespace collapsed, trimmed). New supabase/functions/photo-search: pexels.ts (searchPexels: GET /v1/search with per_page 20 and orientation landscape, the key in Authorization, an 8 s AbortSignal timeout; thumb = src.medium, full = src.large2x; it drops photos with a non-https URL or a missing field; hasMore = next_page, false on page 10; Pexels 429 → rate_limited, anything else → failed). handler.ts: no key → unavailable before anything is read or counted; cache read; checkLimit(cached); a hit answers without Pexels; Pexels 429 → 503 unavailable; failures → search_failed; answers are cached, empty ones too, and a failed save doesn't fail the search. request.ts: 400/405/413/429/502/503 with Retry-After 3600/500, and PHOTO_SEARCH_DAILY_CAP defaults to 3,000. Also store.ts and index.ts (PEXELS_API_KEY). Pexels gets the query's cache key, so cached and fresh answers match. Migration 20261101120000_photo_search.sql: private.photo_search_usage, photo_search_daily_total and photo_search_cache. check_photo_search_limit(user, cap, p_cached default false) allows 20 a minute and 100 a day; with p_cached the global cap is neither checked nor counted. get_photo_search_cache (24 hours) and save_photo_search_cache (upsert) exist because the cache is in `private`, which PostgREST can't reach. All three are service role only. One cron job, photo-search-retention, prunes usage rows and cache rows over 24 hours old. config.toml lists only photo-cleanup (for verify_jwt off), so the function isn't registered there, and 8.3 has no function list. .env.example has PEXELS_API_KEY= and PHOTO_SEARCH_DAILY_CAP=. Spec 6.11 is written (flow, errors, cost). Tests: Deno photo-search/handler.test.ts (16, fake Pexels) covers the mapping and request params; the query key and page; non-https and incomplete photos dropped and an empty alt as null; hasMore, page 10 included; no results, cached; a cache hit not calling Pexels or counting the global cap; another page not being a hit; a hit still rate-limited; the user limit; the global cap; no key; Pexels 429; Pexels 500, 401, a network error, HTML and no photos; the timeout; a failed cache save; bad queries and pages refused before counting. request.test.ts (8) covers trimming and the default page, bounds, 405, the 400s, 413 declared or not, failure statuses and Retry-After, 401 from the deployed function, and the cap env. pgTAP 40_photo_search.test.sql (33) covers the minute and day limits with cache hits, the global cap counting only fresh searches, a refused search using no budget, the cache (save, replace, 24-hour expiry, per page), the retention job and access. Jest src/shared/__tests__/photo-search.test.ts covers schema bounds, refusals, photoSearchKey and the shared index. Deno photo-search: 24 passed (needs --allow-sys, as the other functions do, because an npm dependency reads the hostname). supabase db reset and supabase test db pass (40 files, 1091 tests). jest 4577 passed; lint and tsc clean.
- Search photos section (2026-10-17, spec 4.5). New src/features/recipes/photo-search.ts has searchPhotos(query, page = 1), which returns `{ ok, photos, hasMore }` or an error: offline, rate_limited or failed. It works like loadPagePhotos: offline, no session or no config is offline; FunctionsFetchError or a throw is offline; rate_limited, or a bare 429, is rate_limited; everything else, including a 503 with no key and a bad response, is failed. An empty query or a page outside 1–10 fails without a request, and page 10 never has more. photoSearchQuery trims and cuts to 100 characters, and appendPhotos adds a page while dropping ids already shown. In photo.tsx, a "Search photos" group sits between From the page and the Photo library card. It has a TextField (return key search, VoiceOver "Search photos", pre-filled with the recipe's name). The opening search starts in a loading state set in useState, and an effect sends the request, so lint's set-state-in-effect rule is satisfied. After that only Return searches. Each search bumps a run counter, so a late answer to an older one is dropped. The results use the same Grid with a new SearchTile: 4:3, radius md, the thumb, and the author on a surface chip in caption inkSecondary. VoiceOver reads "Photo by X, i of n", or "{alt}, photo by X, i of n" when Pexels gives a description. Load more (Button, busy while loading) asks for the next page of the searched query, not the edited text. A failed Load more keeps the photos and shows the error, and it can be tapped again. "Photos provided by Pexels" is a footnote link that opens https://www.pexels.com through openRecipeLink. The states: placeholders labelled "Searching photos"; offline (known, or from the search) disables the field with "Needs a connection"; no results says "No photos for “X”. Try another word."; rate_limited and failed show their messages. A broken thumb leaves the grid. Tiles don't do anything when tapped yet (onPick is unset); the next task wires picking. Strings are in en/es, and spec 4.5 describes the section. Tests: new search-photos.test.ts (17) covers the request body, the default page and the cut, page 10, bad input with no request, offline three ways, fetch errors and throws, rate_limited by code and status, 503/502/400/500 as failed, a bad response, photoSearchQuery and appendPhotos. recipe-photo-sheet.test.tsx "Search photos" (17) covers the search on open with the name and placeholders; the section's place; a long name cut; tile and chip style; typing not searching while Return does; an empty Return; a stale answer dropped; Load more paging, busy, de-duplicated and hidden on the last page; no Load more; a failed Load more then a retry; offline two ways; no results; rate_limited and failed; a broken tile; and the Pexels link. I changed four existing assertions in recipe-photo-sheet.test.tsx because the sheet now has this section: three texts() lists gain "Search photos" and "Photos provided by Pexels", and the offline count of "Needs a connection" goes from 2 to 3. The expo-linking mock there is now a spy. jest 4611 passed, lint and tsc clean.
- Picking a stock photo, and its credit (2026-10-17, spec 4.5). photo-upload.ts: the upload body moved into a private runUpload(recipeId, shown, credit, stock, prepare). uploadRecipePhoto uses it and works the same. The new uploadStockPhoto(recipeId, photo) downloads `full` with expo-file-system's File.downloadFileAsync to a new `stock-<uuid>.jpg` in Paths.cache (idempotent), runs downscalePhoto(…, UPLOAD_PHOTO), deletes the full-size download (also when shrinking fails), and uploads with stockPhotoCredit(photo) ({ provider: 'pexels', author, authorUrl, pageUrl }). PhotoUpload has a new `stock` field. While a stock photo downloads, `localUri` is its `thumb`, so the recipe screen shows the thumb with the spinner. retryPhotoUpload re-runs the whole pick (download included) when `stock` is set. In the Choose a photo sheet, tapping a search result calls uploadStockPhoto and closes the sheet; SearchSection's onPick and SearchTile's onPress are now required. In recipe-credit.tsx, the new PhotoCreditChip reads "Photo: Jane Doe / Pexels" in caption inkSecondary on a surface chip at the bottom right, opens pageUrl through openRecipeLink, and VoiceOver reads "Photo by Jane Doe on Pexels" as a link with a hint. RecipePhotoHeader takes a `credit` and shows the chip in place of the source credit, and no credit while uploading. The recipe screen passes useRecipePhoto's credit, so the site's source credit still never shows on an own photo, and rows and plan tiles are unchanged. New en/es strings are under recipes.photoCredit. Spec 4.5 now describes the pick and replaces the no-credit rule with the own-photo credit rule. - Names on the "Added" rows (2026-10-17, spec 4.4). `addedByLabel` and `addedLine` in src/domain/added-by.ts take the household's short names as an optional last argument (`names`, defaulting to empty, as `checkedLine` does). Another member's add reads "Alex, Tuesday". "Someone else" now covers only a member whose name isn't on the device and an old row with no `added_by`. A recipe add by another member reads "Shakshuka · Alex, yesterday" (new string `addedBy.recipeBy` in en/es). Yours, or one with no `added_by`, stays "Shakshuka, yesterday", and Siri stays "Siri". In the item sheet, each "Added" row has a new `AddedAvatar` before the who-and-when text, at the new token `sizes.addedAvatar` (24). For a member it's a round avatar: their initial ("?" when their name isn't known) on `avatarColors(memberHue(id))`, yours too, or grey for a row with no `added_by`. A recipe add gets an `IconTile` (`recipeHue`, `fork.knife`; grey once the recipe id is gone), and Siri gets a grey `mic` tile. The avatar is hidden from VoiceOver. The recipe row's gap went from xs to sm, to match the mockup's 8 pt. Spec 4.4's "Added" bullet and DESIGN.md's component sizes are updated. Tests: added-by.test.ts has a new addedByLabel case (a member's name, and a recipe add by them) and a new "with the household's names" block (7 cases): a known member, an unknown one, names not loaded, no added_by, you with your name cached, a recipe add by someone else / by you / with none recorded / by an unknown member / once the recipe is gone, Siri asked by another member, and signed out. item-screen.test.tsx has a new "who added each one" block (5 cases): rows named You / Alex / Someone else, with 24 pt round avatars "J", "A", "?" in each member's colour and the onPrimary initial; avatars hidden from VoiceOver; an old add with no one recorded showing a grey "?"; a recipe add by Alex ("Shakshuka · Alex, just now", in the row's VoiceOver label too) with its recipe tile, and a Siri row with a grey mic tile; your own recipe add as just the recipe. - "Added by" under the item's title (2026-10-17, spec 4.4, board A). New pure `addedBySummary(contributions, userId, names, now)` in src/domain/added-by.ts. It returns `{ people, text, label }`. `people` is up to three `{ userId, initial }` (others by first add, you last). `text` is runs of `{ text, bold }`, with the known names bold. These are runs rather than a single bold substring like notifications use, because there can be two bold names. `label` is the VoiceOver reading ("Added by Alex, Tuesday"). Siri and recipe adds count as their `added_by`. Adds with no added_by, or a `deletedAt`, are skipped, and it returns null when none are left. A member whose name isn't known is "someone" (not bold) with a "?" avatar. Three or more people name the first two and count the rest, so three people read "Alex, Sam and 1 other". New strings addedBy.byOne/byYou/byTwo/byOneAndYou/byMany_one|_other/byOneLabel/byYouLabel/byNobody in en and es. In Spanish, you is "por ti", and the plural is "y 1 persona más" / "y N personas más". `avatarInitial` moved to src/domain/avatar-initial.ts so the domain stays free of db imports; households.ts re-exports it. New tokens are `sizes.addedByAvatar` 22, `addedByAvatarRing` 2 and `addedByAvatarOverlap` 6. The item sheet has a new `AddedByRow` between the amount and the Checked line: overlapping avatars ringed in `background`, then a footnote inkSecondary line with nested ink 700 names, as one accessible element. Spec 4.4 has a new "Added by" bullet. DESIGN.md has the sizes and an "Added by row" component pattern. - Push tokens and notification settings (2026-10-17, spec 4.13, 5). New migration supabase/migrations/20261103120000_push_notifications.sql. public.push_tokens (token pk, user_id cascade + index, platform ios|android, created_at/updated_at with the set_updated_at trigger) has RLS on and no client grants. register_push_token(p_token, p_platform) and unregister_push_token(p_token) are security definer, granted to authenticated only, and raise `not signed in` (42501) without a user. register checks the shape `^(Exponent|Expo)PushToken\[[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\]$` and ≤ 200 chars (`invalid token`, 22023) and the platform (`invalid platform`), then upserts on token: another user's token moves to the caller and updated_at is bumped. unregister deletes only the caller's row and is a no-op otherwise. public.notification_settings (user_id pk cascade, items_added/shopping default true, quiet_hours_start/end, time_zone default 'UTC') checks the quiet hours are both null or both set and different, has a trigger (private.check_notification_time_zone) raising `invalid time zone` (22023) for a name not in pg_timezone_names, and select/insert/update own-row policies (no delete). private.push_recipients(household, actor, kind, at) returns (user_id, token, platform), one row per device, for current members other than the actor, with the kind on (`items_added` | `shopping`, else `invalid kind`) and outside quiet hours [start, end) in their own time zone (start > end wraps midnight); a missing settings row is the defaults. Execute is service_role only; the migration grants service_role usage on schema private. Note for the worker task: `private` isn't exposed by PostgREST (config.toml schemas = public, graphql_public), so supabase-js `.rpc` can't reach push_recipients as is; the worker needs a direct Postgres connection or a public service_role-only wrapper. Spec 5 updated: both tables in the DDL, the cascade note, and RLS bullets for notification_settings, push_tokens and push_recipients (4.13 itself comes with the docs task). Tests: new supabase/tests/41_push_notifications.test.sql (56): privileges on the functions and tables; register, the ExpoPushToken shape, bad shapes (no prefix, empty, a space, trailing text, null, too long), a bad platform, not signed in; re-register by the same user (bumps updated_at, keeps created_at) and by another user (moves the token, new platform, still one row); unregistering someone else's and an unknown token (no-op) and one's own; settings RLS (can't read, insert for, update or delete another's row), the column defaults, an unknown time zone on insert and update, half-set and zero-length quiet hours; push_recipients excluding the actor, a former member, a kind switched off and a user with no token, one row per device, quiet hours inside, outside, wrapping midnight (late evening and early morning) and at the exact start and end, across New York and Tokyo, an unknown kind, and authenticated refused; admin.delete_user cascading the user's tokens and settings. supabase db reset and supabase test db (all 41 files, incl. 15_admin and 21_delete_account), jest 4671 passed, lint and tsc clean. No existing tests changed. BY HAND (with the rest of the section): `supabase db push`.
- Push registration in the app (2026-10-17, spec 4.13). `yarn expo install expo-notifications` (~57.0.23) and a plain `"expo-notifications"` in the app.json plugins (no sounds, no background remote notifications; I checked the options in the installed plugin's types because WebFetch wasn't allowed). The install nested expo-constants 57.0.22, so I moved expo-constants to ~57.0.22 and ran `yarn dedupe expo-constants`. expo-doctor's duplicate check now passes; its only failure is the patch mismatches that were already there (expo, @expo/ui, expo-router, expo-linking, expo-crypto, expo-splash-screen, jest-expo). New src/features/push/registration.ts. registerForPush({ prompt }) returns 'unavailable' on web, on a simulator (Device.isDevice) and when signed out. On Android it sets the `default` channel first. It asks only when prompt is true and the status is undetermined. When granted, it gets the Expo token with the EAS projectId (Constants.expoConfig.extra.eas.projectId). It calls register_push_token({ p_token, p_platform }) only when the token or user changed or the last registration is more than 7 days old (needsRegistration), and skips the call if the account changed while the token was fetched. It never throws, and a refused RPC isn't stored, so the next run retries. Also getPushPermission(), requestPushPermission() (= registerForPush({ prompt: true }), returns the status) and startPushRegistration(). That one is wired in AppDataGate (app-data.tsx) per signed-in user: it runs now, on each foreground and on addPushTokenListener, always with prompt false. New src/features/push/stored-registration.ts keeps `push.registration` ({ token, userId, at }) in device settings. Its unregisterPush (2 s timeout) calls unregister_push_token for the stored token, best effort, then always clears the key. It's a separate module so auth/account.ts can import it without a cycle (registration.ts imports getAccount). account.ts signOut() awaits unregisterPush() before supabase.auth.signOut(). Tests: new push/__tests__/registration.test.ts (29): needsRegistration (new token or user, the 7-day edge, a bad date); registering with the projectId and storing it; no prompt when prompt is false; a prompt when asked, accepted and declined; denied isn't asked again or registered; an unchanged token isn't re-sent but is re-sent after 8 days; a new user's token and a new token are sent; a refused RPC isn't stored and is retried; a failed token fetch doesn't throw; sign-out during the token fetch; early returns when signed out, on a simulator and on web; the Android channel before the token, and none on iOS; getPushPermission; startPushRegistration on start, on foreground only (not active → active), on a new token, stop, and a no-op on a simulator; unregisterPush clearing the key on success, on an RPC error, on a rejection and on a hung RPC (timeout), no RPC with nothing stored, and an unreadable stored value. sign-out.test.ts +3: unregister runs after the outbox push and before signOut and clears the key; a failed unregister still signs out and wipes; no RPC when the device never registered. Changed existing test: account.test.ts now mocks `@/db/client` with the test DB, because signOut reads device settings; no assertions changed. jest 4703 passed, lint and tsc clean. BY HAND: `yarn dlx eas-cli credentials` → iOS → production → Push Notifications, then a new development build. NEEDS A MANUAL CHECK: see manual-checks.md.
- Queue household events (2026-10-17, spec 4.13, 8.5). New migration supabase/migrations/20261104120000_household_events.sql: pgmq extension, `pgmq.create('household_events')`, RLS on the queue and archive tables with no policies (as stash's capture_queue). Two security definer trigger functions in private (execute revoked from public, anon, authenticated): `queue_items_added` (after insert on list_item_contributions when added_by is set and deleted_at is null; payload kind, household_id, list_id looked up from the item, actor, contribution_id, list_item_id, recipe_id, source, at = created_at) and `queue_shopping` (after update of checked_at on list_items, null to set with checked_by; payload kind, household_id, list_id, actor, list_item_id, at = checked_at). Each pgmq.send sits in an exception block that raises a warning, so the write never fails. Confirmed that merges in apply_mutation (latest in 20261021120000_combine_languages.sql) re-point contributions with an update, so they queue nothing. Spec: section 4.13 didn't exist yet (earlier tasks only referred to it), so I added it from the goal, plus 8.5 (the queue), and pointed 11.1's pipeline bullet at them. New pgTAP supabase/tests/42_household_events.test.sql (30 asserts): queue exists with RLS; clients can't execute the trigger functions; an add through apply_mutations (exact payload); a direct recipe add (3 contributions give 3 events with recipe_id, at = created_at); a Combine into an open item; a check (exact payload); no event for a re-check, an uncheck, another field's edit, a null checked_by, a null added_by, an already-deleted contribution, an undo and redo of an add, or an item created already checked; an uncheck that merges into an open item (contribution re-pointed, no event); a new item merged within a batch (exactly one event, for the target item); a check through apply_mutations; and after pgmq.drop_queue, an add, a check and a synced add all still succeed and are kept. supabase db reset, supabase test db (42 files), yarn jest (4703), lint and tsc pass. - send-notifications worker (2026-10-17, spec 4.13, 5, 8.5, 11.1). New Edge Function supabase/functions/send-notifications/ (index.ts wiring with `withSupabase({ auth: 'secret' })` as photo-cleanup does, handler.ts, store.ts, expo.ts, testing.ts fakes; `verify_jwt = false` in config.toml). New migration 20261105120000_send_notifications.sql: `private.push_sent` and `private.push_receipts` (RLS on, no client access); public functions for service_role only, because neither pgmq nor private is exposed: read/archive/release_household_events, notification_group (re-reads a group: list name, the actor's first word, live contributions or still-checked items, last push for actor+list+kind), notification_recipients (wraps private.push_recipients), record_push_sent, delete_push_tokens, save/due/delete_push_receipts; `private.request_send_notifications()` posting through pg_net with Vault `project_url` + `notifications_worker_key`, only when events or receipts are pending; cron `send-notifications` every minute and `push-sent-retention` daily (7 days). Two decisions to flag. (1) Groups that aren't ready are released (`pgmq.set_vt` 0) instead of left hidden for the 2-minute timeout: otherwise a burst read across two runs splits into two pushes. Sent/stale/empty events are archived; a group whose send fails stays hidden and is retried until it goes stale. (2) The shopping window is "no shopping push for this actor+list in the last 2 hours" (from push_sent). Wording: items are named and counted once even when a Combine added twice; the recipe form counts contributions; a missing name reads "Someone". AGENTS.md documents seeding the local Vault (host.docker.internal:55321); `EXPO_ACCESS_TOKEN` added to .env.example, commented out. Spec: 8.5 describes the worker, 5 the new tables and functions, and 11.1 now covers only location reminders and per-household muting. WebFetch and curl weren't allowed, so the Expo request/ticket/receipt shapes and the `threadId` field come from memory, not the docs (flagged in manual-checks.md). - Receiving notifications (2026-10-17, spec 4.11, 4.13). New src/features/push/receiving.ts. `listIdFromUrl` accepts only `/list/<uuid>`. `handlePushResponse` acts on each response id once (ids kept in memory). A list not on the device gets `pullNow()` first; if it's still missing, the Lists tab opens and ListScreen shows the toast "That list isn't available" (through `takeListUnavailable`/`onListUnavailable`; new string `lists.toasts.listUnavailable` in en/es). The root layout calls `usePushResponses(ready)` on `useLastNotificationResponse()`, and `startForegroundPushes()` at module load. That handler returns no banner, list, sound or badge, and posts an in-app notification through `foregroundBanner`: new `NotificationKind`s `itemsAdded` (plus, green) and `shopping` (cart, blue), with the push's title and body, the hint "Opens <list>", and an onPress that opens the list. It posts nothing when signed out, or when `markListVisible` says that list is on screen. ListScreen marks itself visible while the pathname is on it (its sheets included), and when opened it runs `dismissListPushes(id)` (getPresentedNotificationsAsync, then dismissNotificationAsync for each with a matching `data.url`, best effort). I added a root `__mocks__/expo-notifications.ts`. Without it, suites that render the list screen load the real module, which warns about Expo Go and throws in getPresentedNotificationsAsync. The push tests still pass their own factories. Spec 4.11 and 4.13 and DESIGN.md's banner line are updated. - Settings → Notifications (2026-10-17, spec 4.13). There's a new "Notifications" row (`bell.badge`, rose) in the Shopping card right after Siri adds to, so it sits between Siri and Aisle order. It opens the new src/app/(tabs)/settings/notifications.tsx, registered in the settings stack. **Permission**: read on open and again on each foreground, so allowing it in iOS Settings shows up on return. Undetermined shows a card ("Stay in the loop" plus the explanation) with Turn on notifications, which calls requestPushPermission(). Denied shows "Notifications are off" with Open Settings (Linking.openSettings). 'unavailable' (simulator/web) gets a one-line note and no button. Granted removes the card. **Settings**, shown only when granted: a "Notify me" card with Items added to lists and Someone starts shopping, and a "Quiet hours" card. Its switch starts at 22:00–07:00. The From/To rows use @expo/ui's SwiftUI `DatePicker` (hourAndMinute, compact, labelsHidden, `disabled` modifier). The app had no time picker, but @expo/ui was already installed, so no new dependency. A start equal to the end saves nothing, shows "Quiet hours need different start and end times." and remounts the pickers so they show the saved time again. A footnote shows the time zone. **Store**: new src/features/push/settings.ts with fetch/save (upsert on user_id of the whole row plus `time_zone` = Intl…timeZone), HH:MM ↔ Postgres time helpers, and a useSyncExternalStore store. A change shows at once; a failed save puts it back and the screen alerts "Couldn't save your notification settings". startTimeZoneSync() runs in AppDataGate per signed-in user: on start and on every foreground it upserts only `time_zone` when it differs from the zone last saved on this device (device setting `push.timeZone` = { userId, timeZone }). **Offline**: the screen follows expo-network. Offline, every switch and picker is disabled with a centred "Needs a connection" (the same wording as the photo sheet's offline note; there was no settings-specific one). Settings that failed to load show the defaults, disabled, and reload once back online. **Ask once**: new src/features/push/ask.ts (in-memory pending moment, device setting `push.asked`) and push-ask-prompt.tsx (a Modal sheet: bell tile, "Turn on notifications?", the explanation, Turn on / Not now), mounted in the root layout when signed in. The moments are a successful accept on the join screen (not alreadyMember) and joinMyInvite, plus refreshMemberNames when the cache had the household with only you and now has someone else (householdMateJoined). A sign-in or a newly pulled household fills the cache from empty, so it never counts. The prompt shows only while the permission is undetermined and marks push.asked as it appears, so it never comes back whatever the answer. push.asked isn't in KEPT_DEVICE_SETTINGS, so a sign-out clears it, but the prompt still won't show once the OS permission was asked. New token `Opacity.scrim` (0.6) is for the prompt's backdrop. New en/es strings: settings.notifications, settings.notificationsScreen.*, pushAsk.*. Spec 4.13 has new "Settings > Notifications" and "Asking once" bullets. DESIGN.md has a "Notifications settings" pattern. New push/__tests__/notifications-screen.test.tsx (16): undetermined → Turn on → switches; undetermined → declined; denied with Open Settings; denied → allowed on foreground; granted with loaded values; unavailable; each switch's exact upsert; a failed save reverting plus the alert; the time zone sent per save (Mexico City then Tokyo); quiet hours on (22:00/07:00, picker props), an edited start, start = end refused then a valid end saving, off → nulls; offline disabling and re-enabling; opened offline → defaults disabled → loaded online. New push/__tests__/ask.test.tsx (12): householdMateJoined cases; notePushMoment ignored once asked; the prompt not showing at launch; showing at a moment with its text and marking push.asked; Turn on requesting; Not now not requesting and never again; once across remounts; not shown for granted/denied/unavailable; a moment noted before mount. Added to existing suites (no existing tests changed): member-names.test.ts +4 (a mate joining a solo household; filling from empty; a third member or a failure; never after asked), my-invites.test.ts +1 (a join is a moment, a failed one isn't), household-screens.test.tsx +2 (join screen: a join is a moment; already a member isn't), settings-screens.test.tsx +1 (the row's place and route). - Item photos, Server (2026-10-17, spec 4.4, 5). New migration supabase/migrations/20261106120000_item_photos.sql: `list_items.photo_path text` with check `list_items_photo_path_shape` (`<household_id>/<list_item_id>/<uuid>.jpg`, lowercase, first folder the row's household and second its own id). apply_mutations needed no change: list_items has no settable whitelist, so every non-key column can be set and is merged per field. Private bucket `item-photos` (jpeg, 2 MB, also in supabase/config.toml) with member select/insert/delete on the first folder and no update policy. Cleanup uses a sibling queue `private.item_photo_deletions`, which was simpler than adding a bucket column and changing the recipe claim's return type. Triggers on list_items (update of photo_path, delete) and on households delete fill it; service-role-only `claim_item_photo_deletions` / `complete_item_photo_deletions` drain it with the same claim logic, so an object an item points at again after Undo is dropped. `private.request_recipe_photo_cleanup()` is replaced so the existing 15-minute cron job fires when either queue has entries. photo-cleanup: new `cleanUpBuckets(stores)` in handler.ts sweeps each bucket (a failing bucket doesn't stop the next; the first error is thrown after), and index.ts builds one store per bucket (recipe-photos, item-photos). The spec 5 list_items block now documents the column, bucket and queue.
