- Item photos, App (2026-10-17, spec 4.4, 4.6, 7). Local: `list_items.photo_path` in src/db/schema.ts and migration 0023_item_photo_path (drizzle-kit generate); pull and the outbox are generic over the schema, so it syncs and merges per field with no sync code changes. **Shared photo code**: recipe-photo-source.ts now has `ownPhotoSource(bucket, path, session)` (authenticated URL, path as `cacheKey`, bearer header) and `usePhotoFallback(key, own)` (one retry with a refreshed token on 400/401/403, then fallback); `recipePhotoSource`/`useRecipePhoto` use them, behaviour unchanged. photo-upload.ts: new `pickPhoto(from)` returns the photo full size; `pickRecipePhoto` is pickPhoto + downscale, as before. **New src/features/lists/item-photos.ts**: `ITEM_PHOTOS_BUCKET`, `itemPhotoPath`, `itemPhotoSource`, `useItemPhoto`, `uploadItemPhoto(itemId, localUri)` (downscale `UPLOAD_PHOTO`, upload to `item-photos` at a new path, then `setItemPhoto`, then the change for "Photo updated"), `retryItemPhotoUpload`, `removeItemPhoto(itemId)`, `undoItemPhotoChange`, upload/change stores like the recipe's. items.ts: `setItemPhoto`, `getItemPhoto`, `itemPhotoQuery`, and `photoPath` in `listItemsQuery`/`ItemView` (optional on the type). **New item-photo.tsx** (`ItemPhotoSection`, under the title block, above the Amount card): no photo → dashed 64 pt Add photo tile, an @expo/ui `Menu` of Take photo / Choose from library; a photo → `Card` 8 pt in, 4:3, radius md, with an @expo/ui `ContextMenu` (long-press) and a round … `Menu`, both Replace photo (submenu of the two sources) and Remove photo (destructive); uploading → the local image with a spinner and no menus; a photo that won't load → `photo` glyph placeholder, menus kept. Tap → full-screen viewer: a `Modal` with a native zooming ScrollView (pinch to 4×; an unzoomed pull down of 80 pt closes, `viewerDragCloses`), a close button, VoiceOver escape. I used the ScrollView's zoom rather than gesture-handler/reanimated: it's native and has fewer moving parts. Toasts ("Photo updated"/"Photo removed" + Undo, "Couldn't upload the photo" + Try again) render inline right under the photo rather than at the sheet's bottom, because the item sheet is a formSheet with detents and a bottom-pinned sibling of its ScrollView risks the iOS 26 zero-height bug noted in themed-view.tsx. **Offline** (new shared hook src/hooks/use-online.ts, moved out of settings/notifications.tsx, which now imports it): the tile is disabled with "Needs a connection"; Replace photo is a disabled menu item with "Needs a connection" as its subtitle, and VoiceOver's replace actions go; Remove still works offline (it's a local, synced write). VoiceOver: "Photo of za'atar" (the stored name), hint "Opens it full screen", custom actions Take photo / Choose from library / Remove photo. **Row**: a `photo` glyph (iconSmall, inkSecondary) after the name, before any NEW badge; the label gets "has a photo" (`itemRowLabel` takes a `photo` part). A reopen by a new add keeps the row, so it keeps the photo. `clearPhotoCache` clears all of expo-image's caches, so it already covers item photos (comment updated). Tokens: `sizes.itemPhotoTile` 64, `sizes.itemPhotoMore` 32, `PhotoViewer.background` (#000000, `useTheme().photoViewer`); DESIGN.md has them and an "Item photo" pattern. Strings: `item.photo.*`, `lists.row.hasPhoto` in en/es. Spec: an item-photo bullet in 4.4, a row note in 4.6, a sync note in 7.1 (5 was done by the server task). Not handled: moving an item onto a list where it Combines or reopens another item drops the moved item's photo (the target keeps its own); the task didn't cover Move. Tests: new src/db/__tests__/item-photo-migration.test.ts (4: tag, no column before, existing item gets null, nullable text takes a path and a null); new lists/__tests__/item-photos.test.ts (20: path shape; source URL/cacheKey/header, signed out, none; pickPhoto full size, camera permission, cancel; upload shrinks, bucket, path, photo_path + outbox, change; Replace + Undo to the old path; failure + Try again; unreadable photo; toast keys; item deleted before/while uploading; Remove + Undo; nothing to remove; setItemPhoto writes only changes and refuses a deleted item; groupItems carries photoPath; reopen keeps it); new lists/__tests__/item-photo-sheet.test.tsx (22: the tile and its place, its menu's two items, library upload with spinner then the stored photo + toast, Undo, camera denied then allowed, cancel, failure + Try again, 4:3 card + VoiceOver label/hint/actions, both menus' items, Replace + Undo, Remove + Undo, VoiceOver remove and library actions, the full-screen viewer (zoom props, label, close, short vs long pull), 401 retry then fallback, a photo that won't load, offline tile and coming back online, offline Replace disabled + Remove works, reopen keeps it, a pulled photo appears, Spanish, `viewerDragCloses`); item-row.test.tsx +3 (glyph + label, glyph before NEW, none/null/Spanish); pull.test.ts +4 (path applied, newer remote clear, local unpushed photo beats an older remote one, a server without the column). Existing tests changed because this task changes what they check: write-paths.test.ts (a new item's outbox insert now has `photo_path: null`); item-screen.test.tsx (its mocks gained `Button`/`ContextMenu`/`disabled`, a reanimated mock for the Toast and an expo-network mock, and the Spanish unit-menu test now picks the unit menu out of the sheet's two native menus). yarn jest (4835 passed, 2 skipped), lint and tsc clean. - Import closes right away (2026-10-17, spec 4.10, 4.11). src/app/import-recipe.tsx: Save first checks what fails at once and shows it inline with the sheet open: "That doesn't look like a link" (as before) and "You're offline" (new, from `useOnline`/expo-network; an invalid link wins over offline). Otherwise it starts the import, calls `router.back()` and posts the new `importingNotification(id)` (src/features/recipes/import-notifications.ts: `recipeImport`, `fork.knife`, `recipeHue(id)`, "Importing your recipe" / "We'll let you know when it's ready.", no onPress). The id comes from the new `importNotificationId()` (`recipe-import-<uuidv7>`, one per import). There's no recipe id yet, so the Importing tile's hue comes from that id, not the recipe's. The result always takes the old closed-while-reading path, posted with that same id so it replaces the Importing banner (or queues after whatever is showing): "Recipe added" (tap → the list and its toast with Undo), "Recipe saved" (tap → the recipe; this now applies from a list's menu too, where the toast used to show on that list), or "Couldn't import recipe" (tap → the sheet with the same choices, or Add recipe for photos). The device fallback runs inside importRecipe, so it still comes before a link failure. A ref stops a second Save before the sheet goes. Removed: the `open` ref, the `working` state and every disabled/editable it drove, the "Reading recipe…" label, the "You can close this…" line, the inline branch for server answers, and `recipes.import.reading` / `recipes.import.canClose` in en.ts and es.ts (nothing else used them; docs/i18n/es-review.csv is a generated export and still lists them until it's regenerated). New strings `notifications.importing` / `importingBody` (en, es). The builders keep their own default ids (failure by link, 'recipe-import-failed-photo'), and the sheet overrides them. Spec: in 4.10, "From inside the app" (a Save bullet replaces the reading bullet and the switch-off toast bullet), "From a photo", the in-app failure bullet, and "When the sheet was closed while reading", renamed "When the import finishes"; also 4.11's kinds line. Tests: in recipe-import-screens.test.tsx the expo-network mock now has `getNetworkStateAsync`, driven by `mockConnected`. Existing tests changed because the behaviour they check changed: the "while reading…" test is now "Save closes the sheet at once and posts Importing" (exact notification, no onPress, no reading state, a second submit does nothing); success → "Recipe added" with the same id, and on tap the list, toast and Undo; from a list's menu, success is the notification too; both switch-off tests now expect "Recipe saved" (tap opens the recipe) instead of a toast; the inline-failure test.each is now "a server failure replaces Importing with its reason" (plus blocked and rate_limited); the closed-while-reading success and recipe-only tests are folded into those; the failure-reopens tests read the second notify call. All the photo tests changed the same way (Importing, then Recipe added/saved; the failure wording with the same id; tap opens Add recipe). New: each import gets its own id; offline stays inline and posts nothing (link and photo), and typing clears it; an invalid link while offline says it isn't a link; the invalid-link test also checks there's no back or notify. import-notifications.test.ts +3: importingNotification's exact shape, Spanish, and importNotificationId's format and uniqueness. yarn jest (4842 passed, 2 skipped), lint and tsc clean. Nothing in supabase/ changed.

- Shelves and an A–Z default, the logic (2026-10-18, spec 4.5, 4.16): new `src/features/recipes/recipe-shelves.ts`. `recipeShelves({ recipes, entries, today, language })` returns This week / Recently added / Favorites as `{ key, title, count, items: { recipe, caption }[] }`. This week takes `kind: 'recipe'` entries whose recipe is in `recipes`, from today through the week's Saturday, sorted by day, then `MEALS` order, then position. Each recipe shows once, at its first slot, captioned by `shortSlotName`, and `count` is the number of those entries. Recently added is the first 10 of `sortRecipes(…, 'recent')` and counts every recipe. Favorites is `sortRecipesByName` of recipes whose known Saved tags hold `favorite`, up to 10 (`SHELF_MAX_ITEMS`), and counts every favorite. Captions come from `recipeTimeLabel`. Empty shelves are dropped, and there are none below `SHELVES_MIN_RECIPES` = 8. `shelvesShown({ query, filters, recipeCount })`: a blank or whitespace query isn't a search; any `activeFilterCount` (Untagged too) hides them. Titles are `recipes.shelves.*` (en "This week"/"Recently added"/"Favorites", es "Esta semana"/"Agregadas recientemente"/"Favoritas"), read with `lng: language`. `DEFAULT_RECIPE_SORT` is now `name`; a stored value is kept. Spec not touched: the next task updates 4.5/4.16. New tests: recipe-shelves.test.ts (17). They cover shelf order, captions and counts; the 10-item cap with full counts; past days, last week and next week left out, Saturday kept; a recipe planned twice shows once and both entries count; position order inside a slot; notes, leftovers, eating out and missing recipes left out; empty shelves left out, and want-to-try isn't a favorite; spanning households; fewer than 8 gives none; Spanish titles and "cena del jue"; `shelvesShown` for count, search, blank search, and tag/time/Untagged filters; `getRecipeSortChoice()` reading A–Z with nothing stored and keeping a stored sort. The flow is plan, then kill switches (description model or `AI_IMAGE_MODEL=off`, 503 before reserving), then reserve (404/409/503), read (name, live ingredient display names, known cuisine labels only), describe, generate, upload to `<household>/<recipe>/<uuid>.jpg`, complete, `200 { path }`. - Portal functions for plans (2026-10-18, spec 4.12, 5): DONE, checked off. New migration `20261117120000_admin_plans.sql`: constraint trigger `plan_features_import_needs_categorize` (`after insert or update or delete`, `deferrable initially deferred`, function `private.check_import_needs_categorize`) raising `recipe import needs categorize` (`23514`) when a touched plan (old and new plan_id) has `recipe_import` or `recipe_import_photo` but not `categorize`; it checks the plan as it is now, not the row. Functions (`security definer`, `search_path = ''`, `require_admin()` first, execute only for authenticated): `admin_list_plans()` (by id; `id`, `name`, `is_default`, `users`, `features` [{ feature, monthly_limit }] in `private.feature_keys()` order), `admin_create_plan`, `admin_rename_plan`, `admin_set_default_plan` (details also has `previous_default`), `admin_set_plan_feature`, `admin_set_plan_limit` (`feature not in plan` P0002), `admin_delete_plan` (`default plan` / `plan in use` 23503). Other errors: `plan not found` P0002, `note required` / `name required` / `invalid plan id` 22023, `plan exists` 23505. Each success writes one audit row (`action` without `admin_`, `target_plan_id`, `details` { before, after, users } from the new `private.plan_snapshot` / `private.plan_user_count`) and returns the details. New helpers: `private.log_plan_action` (runs `set constraints … immediate` then `deferred`, so a broken rule raises from the call, not at commit), `private.lock_plan` and `private.require_note`. No client can execute any of them. Two choices the task didn't spell out: Postgres requires every param after a defaulted one to have a default, so `p_note` in `admin_create_plan` and `admin_set_plan_feature` is `default null` and still required (raises `note required`); disabling a feature the plan doesn't have changes nothing but still writes its audit row (as `set_user_plan` does when the plan doesn't change). - The portal's look and shell (2026-10-18, spec 4.12): DONE, checked off. `admin/app/tokens.css`: every `Palette.light`/`Palette.dark` colour as a kebab-case variable named after its token (`inkSecondary` → `--ink-secondary`) under `:root` and `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)`, each aisle hue as `--aisle-<hue>`/`-soft`/`-strong`, and the portal's own sidebar green (`--sidebar-green`, `primary` darkened) with the colours on it; the header says it's copied from `src/constants/theme.ts`. `globals.css` imports it, replaces shadcn's grey `:root`/`.dark` blocks with shadcn variables pointed at the tokens, exposes the tokens as Tailwind colours (`bg-surface`, `text-ink-secondary`, …), makes the `dark` variant follow the system, and sets tabular figures and h1–h3 in Bricolage 800 at −0.025 em. `layout.tsx` loads Bricolage Grotesque (`--font-display`) and Figtree (`--font-body`) through `next/font/google`. `admin/lib/features.ts` holds one `FEATURES` map with each feature's label, unit ("names" for categorize, else "calls") and aisle hue (categorize green, recipe import blue, photo import teal, tags amber, step matching orange, AI photos plum as in 4.5), in the server's order (`private.feature_keys()`, which isn't the app's alphabetical `FEATURES` order, so the drift test compares them as sets). It also exports `FEATURE_KEYS`, `isFeature`, `featureLabel` and `featureColors`. `admin/lib/types.ts`: hand-written result types for every `admin_*` function, taken from the migrations. Shell: route group `app/(portal)/` whose layout puts `components/sidebar.tsx` (mark, name, "Back office" pill, Users/Plans/AI usage/Activity with lucide icons, `aria-current` from `usePathname`) beside the page. Empty `users`, `plans`, `ai-usage` and `activity` pages use `components/page-header.tsx`; `/` redirects to `/users`. The mark is an inline SVG cloche in `--gold` (`components/mark.tsx`), because this session couldn't run `sips` to shrink `splash-cloche.png`. The sample `admin/public/*.svg` files are still there (deleting wasn't allowed). Tests: Vitest `admin/lib/features.test.ts` covers the order, units, distinct hues and labels, `isFeature` (including `toString`/`__proto__`) and the fallbacks for an unknown key; the env test stays. Root Jest `src/constants/__tests__/admin-drift.test.ts` reads the admin files as text and checks that the features map names exactly the app's `FEATURES` and that tokens.css has every light and dark `Palette` and `AisleColors` colour under its variable, with tests of its own parsers. - Change a user's plan (2026-10-19, spec 4.12): DONE, checked off. The Plan card on `/users/[id]` now has a **Change plan** button (`admin/components/change-plan.tsx`, client). It opens a Base UI dialog with the choices as radio cards. **Default (Plus)** comes first, named after `admin_list_plans`' default ("Default" with "No features" when no plan is the default), then every plan by id, each with its features and limits on one line ("Categorize 250 names · Step matching 100 matches", "… no limit", "No features"). The current one is marked **Current**: the default for an unassigned user, otherwise their own plan, even when that's the default plan. A required **Note** (placeholder "Who asked and why"; Save is disabled while it's blank) has the plan-change line under it. Save calls the server action `setUserPlan` (`admin/lib/plan-actions.ts`: `admin_set_user_plan` with the trimmed note, `forbidIfNotAuthorized`, then `refresh()` from `next/cache`, so the page re-renders with the new plan and history in the same round trip). The dialog then closes and "Moved <email> to <choice title>" shows for 5 s in a toast-coloured `role="status"` line (no toast library; I added `--color-toast`/`--color-on-toast` to globals.css). An error stays in the dialog as a `role="alert"` with the server's message. The dialog can't be closed while saving, and reopening it resets to the current plan with an empty note. `lib/supabase/plans.ts` gains `loadPlans` and `planNames`, since the detail page needs the full list; `loadPlanNames` is built from them. The pure wording is in `admin/lib/plan-change.ts` (`planSummary`, `planChoices`, `defaultChoiceTitle`, `movedMessage`, `isNoteValid`). Vitest `admin/lib/plan-change.test.ts` (14 tests): summaries (feature order and units, singular for 1, thousands separator, null and 0 limits, an empty plan, an unknown key); choices (order and titles; current for an unassigned user, their own plan, their own plan when it's the default, a plan missing from the list; no default plan; an empty list); the moved message with and without an email; blank notes. Not run against a dev server or local Supabase. No real AI calls. - AI usage (2026-10-19, spec 4.12): DONE, checked off. The page is at `/ai-usage`, the route the shell task gave it (the sidebar already links there), not the task's `/usage`. `admin/app/(portal)/ai-usage/page.tsx` loads `admin_ai_usage` and `loadPlanNames` in a Suspense (errors go through `forbidIfNotAuthorized`), with the 40-day note under the title. There's one card per feature, in the fixed order. It starts with a tile tinted in the feature's aisle colour (label, this month's total in its unit, "This month · N users"). Then `components/usage-chart.tsx`, a plain SVG of the 40 daily bars in the feature's colour: other days at 55% opacity, today full with a stroke and "Today" under it, an empty day as a 1-unit stub in the soft tint, any use at least 2 units tall, a dashed line before each 1st with its date ("Oct 1") in a band above, a `<title>` per bar and `role="img"`. Then **Top users**: email linking to `/users/[id]`, plan name, and "used / limit" or "used / No limit" ("No one has used it this month." when empty). Below that, **AI calls**, "Last 30 days": a By task table (task, calls, failure rate with each non-`ok` status in its `title`, fallbacks, input / output / cached / reasoning tokens) and a smaller By model table. The SQL gives models per task, so the model table sums each model across tasks and lists the tasks that used it under its name. With no calls the section says "No AI calls in the last 30 days." The pure helpers are in `admin/lib/ai-usage.ts` (`chartBars`, `dayLabel`, `barTitle`, `failureRate`/`failureRateText`, `failureBreakdown`, `usageTotal`, `usersLine`, `usedOfLimit`, `callsByModel`, `count`). - `ai_calls` records who made each call (2026-10-19, spec 5, 6.4, 6.5): DONE, checked off. Migration `20261119120000_ai_calls_caller.sql` adds `user_id` (→ `auth.users`, on delete set null) and `household_id` (→ `households`, on delete set null) with an index on `(user_id, created_at desc)`. `run-ai-task.ts` exports `AiCaller = { userId, householdId | null }`; `AiCallRecord` gains `user_id`/`household_id`, and `RunAiTaskOptions.caller?` is copied onto every attempt (primary and fallback, ok or failed), nulls without it. Wiring: `categorize(names, store, model?, caller?)` (a 4th positional parameter, so the 45 existing `categorize(…, store, model)` calls didn't change); `caller?` on `ImportDeps`, `PhotoImportDeps`, `SuggestDeps`, `MatchDeps`/`MatchStepsDeps` and `DescribeOptions`; `GenerateDeps.userId?`, with the household taken from the reservation. Each `index.ts` passes `{ userId, householdId: null }` (only AI photos have a household), and import-recipe / import-recipe-photo pass it to the inline categorize too. CLIs and evals pass nothing, so their rows have nulls. Updated the "no user id" comments in the stores, spec 5 (server-only tables), 6.4's Privacy bullet, 6.5 (a new paragraph on `ai_calls`), the Privacy lines of 6.6, 6.7, 6.8, 6.10 and 6.11, `docs/admin.md`, and the privacy policy line in `docs/alpha-testing.md` (logged with the account, 30 days, 180 for flagged calls, admins can read it). Existing tests changed because the behavior changed: `06_ai_calls.test.sql` checked `hasnt_column user_id`; it now checks the columns, nullability, the index and the FK, that deleting the user nulls `user_id` and keeps the row and its household, and that deleting the household nulls `household_id` and keeps the user (plan 9 → 20). The suggest-recipe-tags and match-recipe-steps "no user id" tests asserted `!('user_id' in call)`; they now assert the caller's user id and a null household. `categorize-ingredients/cli.test.ts`'s record fixture got the two fields. New Deno tests: `ai.test.ts` (the caller on both the failed primary and the fallback; a caller with no household; no caller gives nulls on ok and on failure), categorize `handler.test.ts` (caller on ok and invalid_output rows; nulls without one), import-recipe and import-recipe-photo (the extraction and inline categorize rows carry the user with a null household; photo import also a failed extraction), suggest-recipe-tags and match-recipe-steps (a failed call carries the caller too), generate-recipe-photo (the description row has the user and the recipe's household, a failed description too, nulls with no caller). Checks: Deno tests in categorize-ingredients (295), import-recipe (177), import-recipe-photo (64), match-recipe-steps (61), suggest-recipe-tags (59), generate-recipe-photo (51), all pass; every edited `index.ts`, CLI and eval type-checks; `supabase db reset` and `supabase test db` (1623 passed); root jest (5205 passed), lint, tsc. No real AI calls.
- Failed AI calls keep the provider's answer (2026-10-19, spec 5, 6.4): DONE, checked off. Migration `20261120120000_ai_calls_error_body.sql` adds `http_status integer` and `error_body text` to `ai_calls`. `AiCallRecord` gains both; a failed attempt takes them from `apiCallError(err)` (an `APICallError`, or a `RetryError`'s last one): `statusCode` and `responseBody` cut to 4000; `error` is unchanged (`name: message`, 2000); ok rows and failures with no HTTP answer have nulls. Safety blocks: the provider source wasn't readable from the sandbox (the Deno npm cache is outside the repo), so I ran the real `@ai-sdk/google@4.0.88`, `@ai-sdk/anthropic@4.0.72` and `@ai-sdk/openai@4.0.96` against a fake `fetch` returning blocked bodies in a throwaway Deno test. Gemini's `promptFeedback.blockReason` (SAFETY, PROHIBITED_CONTENT) and `SAFETY`/`PROHIBITED_CONTENT` candidate finishes, and Anthropic's `stop_reason: 'refusal'`, all come back as finish reason `content-filter` (raw = the provider's reason) with no output, which `runAiTask` already turns into an `invalid_output` row that keeps the finish reason, so no mapping was needed (a comment says so). The task names `@ai-sdk/openai`, but no function imports it: the language-model providers are Google and Anthropic, and OpenAI is only the image call's plain `fetch` (next task). For the record, `@ai-sdk/openai` reports a chat `refusal` as finish `stop` with empty text and drops the refusal; I didn't map that, since mapping "empty + stop" to `content-filter` would wrongly flag other empty answers and the shape can't reach `runAiTask`. Spec 5 (`ai_calls` line) and 6.4 (new "What a failed attempt records" and "Safety blocks" bullets) say all this. Tests: Deno `categorize-ingredients/ai.test.ts` gets an APICallError 529 with a JSON body records both columns and the error text; a long body/error is cut to 4000/2000; no body gives a status and a null body; a RetryError (503 then 529) records the last error's; invalid output, a timeout (`TimeoutError`) and an ok attempt record nulls; five mock shapes reproducing the probed safety blocks (Gemini blocked prompt, prohibited prompt, SAFETY finish, SAFETY after partial JSON, Anthropic refusal) each record `invalid_output` + `content-filter`; a safety block doesn't try the fallback. `cli.test.ts`'s record helper gets the two new fields (type change only). pgTAP `06_ai_calls`: both columns' types, nulls when unset, and a failed row stores 529 and its body (plan 20 → 25). Deno suites: categorize-ingredients (308), import-recipe (177), import-recipe-photo (64), match-recipe-steps (61), suggest-recipe-tags (59), generate-recipe-photo (51), all pass; `supabase db reset` + `supabase test db` pass; root jest (5205 passed), lint, tsc pass. No real AI calls (the probe used a fake fetch). Cleanup needed: the sandbox wouldn't let me delete the scratch files `.probe/probe.ts` and `supabase/functions/categorize-ingredients/zz-probe.test.ts` (emptied); delete both, they aren't part of the change.
- The AI photo's image call goes into `ai_calls` (2026-10-19, spec 6.4, 6.11): DONE, checked off. Migration `20261121120000_ai_calls_image.sql`: `refused` added to the `ai_calls.status` check; `recipe_id uuid references public.recipes on delete set null`; `private.ai_call_stats` counts `refused` (zeros included) and leaves `recipePhotoImage` rows out of `fallbacks`, since their `fallback_of` is the link to the description, not a fallback. I kept `fallback_of` for the link (no `parent_id`), as the next tasks' retention and `primary`/`fallback` build on it; the spec says so. `run-ai-task.ts`: `AiCallRecord` gains `recipe_id`, `task` may be `RECIPE_PHOTO_IMAGE_TASK` (`recipePhotoImage`), status may be `refused`; `RunAiTaskOptions.recipeId?` is copied onto each attempt (null otherwise). `image.ts`: `ImageRefusedError` and `ImageFailedError` carry `httpStatus` and `body` (the raw text of a failed answer; a 200's body is never kept, since it holds the image), and `ImageFailedError.failure` is the row's status (HTTP or network error `api_error`, timeout `timeout`, no image/bad base64 `invalid_output`, no key/`off` `error`); new `imageModelId` (`openai:<model>`, `mock` stays `mock`). `describe.ts` takes `recipeId` and returns the answering row's `callId`. `handler.ts` writes one `recipePhotoImage` row per image call (success, refusal, failure) with the caller, household, recipe, empty instructions, the image prompt, `{ quality, path }` on success (the row is written before the upload), tokens, duration, `fallback_of` the description row, and `error`/`http_status`/`error_body` (4000) on failure. A failed write logs `ai_call_log_failed` and never fails the request. Portal: `AiCallStatus`/`STATUS_ORDER` gain `refused` (between timeout and error). A merge (insert or update with `merged_into`) logs `item.merged` only; contribution hard deletes and re-pointing log nothing; an override delete cascading from its aisle's delete logs nothing. Household functions: `private.set_activity_action` / `clear_activity_action` / `suppress_activity` (the trigger marks the action `logged` after the first write); `create_household`, `delete_household`, `leave_household`, `remove_member`, `rename_household`, `set_combine_languages`, `create_invite` (now picks the invite id up front so the entry can name it), `revoke_invite`, `decline_invite`, `set_member_role` and `private.accept_household_invite` (which serves both accept functions) re-created with the action set after their checks and cleared before returning, otherwise unchanged. `private.delete_user_core` suppresses logging for its own writes, then deletes the user's entries and strips `member`/`member_id` from other users' `member.removed`/`member.role_changed` entries about them. `user-activity-retention` cron at 03:47 UTC deletes rows older than 90 days. `auth.uid()` is the caller in `apply_mutations` (security definer, reads the JWT claims) and the household functions, which the tests check by the logged `user_id`. Migration `20261205120000_admin_user_activity.sql`: `private.user_activity_rows(user, household, from, to)` (security definer, no client execute) unions the user's `private.user_activity` (`source` `app`, `household` the snapshot in `data`, else the household's current name), their `ai_calls` (`ai`, `ai.<task>`, `entity_id` the call id, `data` `{ id, status, flag, recipe_id }`) and the `admin.admin_actions` rows targeting them (`admin`, `admin.<action>`, `entity_id` the action id, `data` `{ details, note, performed_by_email }`, the email looked up when `performed_by` is a user id). `public.admin_user_activity(...)` (`require_admin()` first, execute for authenticated only) filters by areas, full action names, household and dates; orders newest first (ties: app, ai, admin, then newer app id first); clamps the limit to 1–200; adds `invited_email` to `invite.created` from `household_invites` (null once the invite is gone); returns `counts` for all nine areas (zeros included) over user, household and dates only; and on a first page (no `p_before`) writes one `view_user_activity` row (`details` `{ areas, actions, household_id, from, to }`, no note, `performed_by` the admin). It writes that row after building the result, so the view isn't in its own page. `admin_user_detail` is re-created unchanged plus `last_active_at` (the later of the newest user_activity entry and the newest AI call). **Judgement calls:** dates are `p_from` inclusive and `p_to` exclusive (the portal task should send the day after the range's end). Keyset paging is on the timestamp alone, so a page never splits one timestamp (one transaction's entries share `now()`): when the limit falls inside a timestamp, the page stops before it. If one timestamp fills the whole page, it comes back whole even past the limit (otherwise those rows would be skipped or paging would stall), so "at most 200" holds except for a single transaction with more entries than the limit. `next_before` is the page's oldest time. An unknown user's first page still writes its audit row. **Tests added:** pgTAP `63_admin_user_activity.test.sql` (50): privileges; non-admin and anon refused, with no audit row written; the three sources merge in order, with the tie order; exact app/ai/admin entry shapes (no prompt); `invited_email` while the invite exists, null for a gone invite and after deleting one, never copied into the log, and no key on other entries; counts (every area, ignoring areas/actions/before, following household and dates); each filter and their combination; paging by 2 returns all 12 once and in order, with page sizes [2,2,2,2,3,1]; a page stopping before a split timestamp; the limit capped; the first page writes one audit row with the filters and a later page none; an unknown user returns no entries; a deleted user's admin rows are listed; `last_active_at` (app newer than AI, AI alone, null with neither). **Existing test changed (behaviour changed by the task):** `50_admin_users` test 28's exact key list for `admin_user_detail` gains `last_active_at`. **Checks:** jest (5396 passed, 2 skipped), lint, tsc; `supabase db reset`, `supabase test db` (63 files, 2066 tests). Note: running `supabase test db` a second time without a reset fails `44_item_photos` 33, `45_recipe_pages` 35–36 and `46_recipe_photo_ai` 28 (apparently leftover state from the previous run). They pass after a reset and don't touch this change. No app or admin code changed. No real AI calls. **Hosted step for the user (not run):** `supabase db push`.

Migration `20261207120000_household_aisle_order.sql`: `private.log_user_activity()` re-created without the `user_category_order` branch and with branches for the two new tables (otherwise unchanged; the actor is still `auth.uid()`); `private.activity_user_category_order` dropped; the table taken out of `supabase_realtime` and dropped with its data, grants, policy and triggers. `private.check_aisle_order(ids, household)` (security definer, no client execute) raises 22023 `invalid aisle order` for anything but a jsonb array of uuid strings that are distinct (compared as uuids, so case doesn't matter), each a system category or a custom one of that household; an empty array is allowed. A `before insert or update of category_ids, household_id` trigger runs it on both tables. `public.household_category_order` (`household_id` pk, `category_ids`, `updated_by`, `field_clock`, plus `created_at`/`updated_at` with `set_updated_at`, which pull's cursors need; spec 5 says most tables have them): members can select, clients can't write, published to realtime. `private.apply_mutation` gains it: key `household_id`; `category_ids` is the only settable field; a client's `updated_by` is dropped; `updated_by` is set to the caller whenever `category_ids` wins; a new row without `category_ids` is refused with `invalid aisle order`; there's no delete path. `public.user_household_category_order` (`(user_id, household_id)` pk, `category_ids`, timestamps): select/insert/update/delete granted, one RLS policy for all four with `user_id = auth.uid()` and `household_id in my_household_ids()`, published to realtime. `private.remove_member_from_household` deletes the leaver's row, which logs nothing beyond the household function's own entry. Activity: `aisles`/`aisle_order.saved` `{ household }` when the household order is inserted or its `category_ids` changes; `settings`/`settings.aisle_order` `{ household, removed }` for each write of your own order (`removed` true on delete), one entry per row (no once-per-transaction guard: a save is one row now). Old clients: PostgREST answers a request on a dropped table with 404 (`PGRST205`, or `42P01` from a stale schema cache), and `isPermanent` in `push.ts` already counts a 404 as a permanent rejection. On the client, `user_category_order` is out of `USER_TABLE_COLUMNS` and in a new `RETIRED_TABLES`, so a queued change to it is rejected locally (`retired`) without a request. I couldn't check the live 404 (curl needed approval in this session), so that part rests on PostgREST's documented behaviour plus a jest test with a 404 PGRST205 response. - Shared planner, and the app adopts it (plan §1, §5 Planning): DONE, checked off. **New, pure, in `src/shared/domain/`:** `list-plan.ts` with `planListAdds(snapshot, adds, meta, unitKnowledge, ids)` and `import-plan.ts` with `planRecipeImport({ extracted, target, existingRecipe, listSnapshot, unitKnowledge, ids, at, userId })`. `writes` are in the outbox's wire shape without the HLC (`{ op: 'insert' | 'update' | 'upsert', table, id | key, fields, counters? }`, Postgres names, ISO dates), so the server can hand them to `apply_mutation`; `op` is an addition to the plan's shape, because the device needs to know insert from update. `preconditions`: `combine` (once per snapshot item), `reopen` (item, `checkedAt` ISO, live contribution ids), `new` (list, the family key when the household combines languages, else the own key), `recipeAbsent` / `recipeReuse`. Per-add outcomes carry the combine total as amounts + usual unit (the device formats `totalAmountText`, which needs i18n). **Deviations from the plan's types, needed to match `applyAdd` exactly:** `UnitKnowledge` is per name `{ unitUsage, defaultUnit }` (not a resolved unit), so the planner updates unit history as the batch goes, as the sequential device code did (the counted-one rule and the toast's usual unit see earlier adds' units); `ListSnapshot` also has `families` (each batch name's family key), and its items carry `displayName`, `checkedBy` and live contributions with amounts. Ids are taken in the device's old order (contribution before item; recipe, then its ingredients, then the adds). **Device:** new `src/sync/apply-writes.ts` (`applyWrites` runs writes through `insertRow` / `updateRows` / `upsertRow`); `items.ts` has `addSnapshot` (built from SQLite, live items filtered to the batch's names and families) and `addResults` (pending names via `ensureLocalIngredient`, toast total), and `addItem`, `addSiriItem`, `addItemsBulk` all go through `planListAdds`; `applyAdd`/`decideAdd` are gone. The import's `save()` builds the snapshot, calls `planRecipeImport` and applies its writes; `createImportedRecipe`/`liveIngredientIds` are replaced by `newRecipeId` (the import id's recipe id unless taken). Local-only steps (touching the new recipe's `updated_at`, pending catalog rows, `applyResults`) stay in the adapter. One small change: a reopen's soft-deleted amounts now get the add's `at` rather than a second `new Date()` a few ms later. **Tests:** new `src/shared/domain/__tests__/list-plan.test.ts` (new item with exact writes and id order, no-quantity and bare-number units, Combine with total, open over checked, reopen with deletes/preconditions/`reopened`, latest checked and a reopen with no amounts, same name twice in a batch, one `combine` precondition for several adds, twins with combine on/off and family folding, counted-one on/off cases and units learned within the batch, null adds, null user, `knownUnit`) and `import-plan.test.ts` (new recipe: fields, tags with `saved`, positions, trimmed notes, step match update, uses counted once, preconditions, summary; reuse; recipe-only; missing snapshot throws; step mismatch; null tags/empty steps/host name), both also in `supabase/functions/_shared/shared-domain.test.ts` (`deno test -A shared-domain.test.ts`: 19 passed, 77 steps). - Server: per-list lock and activity actor. Migration `20261208121500_list_lock_activity_actor.sql` redefines `private.apply_mutation` (from `household_aisle_order.sql`) to take `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('list:' || list_id, 0))` on every `list_items` write, before the row's own `for update` read (list lock before item locks, so two batches on one list can't deadlock), with the list from the existing row or the fields; an open item moved to another list also locks the new list before the open-item decision. `private.log_user_activity` logs as `coalesce(auth.uid(), nullif(current_setting('app.actor_uid', true), '')::uuid)`. pgTAP `66_list_lock_activity_actor.test.sql` (15 tests) simulates the two transactions by asserting the lock is taken (pg_locks for this backend): a new item, an update by id and a move lock their lists, a `lists` write doesn't; the second open item for a key, written after the first, merges so one stays open; with `app.actor_uid` and no JWT the write is logged for that user; with neither (unset or empty) nothing is logged; with both, `auth.uid()` wins. **Checks:** db reset + `supabase test db` (66 files pass), jest 205 suites (5459 passed, 2 skipped), lint, tsc. One `supabase test db` run in between failed 44–46 because the shared local DB had 40 committed photo-cleanup rows from outside this worktree; after a fresh reset everything passed. No existing test changed. No real AI calls.

Migration `20261209100000_device_credentials.sql` (timestamped before the share-imports task's, whose `credential_id` references it): `public.device_credentials` as in spec 5 (`id` defaults to `gen_random_uuid()`, `expires_at` to now + 90 days, `revoked_reason` checked to `replaced`/`sign_out`/`admin`), the partial unique index `(user_id, installation_id) where revoked_at is null`, RLS on with no policies, nothing granted to anon/authenticated. Four functions: `register_device_credential(p_user_id, p_installation_id, p_token_hash hex)` (service role only; an advisory lock per user+installation, then the live one gets `expires_at = revoked_at = least(expires_at, now() + 24 h)`, `replaced`, and a new row is inserted; returns its `expires_at`; 22023 `invalid credential` for a bad hash); `use_device_credential(p_token_hash, p_scope)` (service role only; one `update … returning user_id` that sets `last_used_at`, so verifying is one atomic statement); `revoke_my_device_credential(p_installation_id)` (authenticated; `sign_out`, now); `admin_revoke_device_credentials(p_user_id, p_note)` (`require_admin()`, `require_note`, `admin`, now, one `revoke_device_credentials` audit row with `{ revoked }`, returned, also for 0). **Choice:** sign-out and admin revocation also revoke a credential still in its 24 h grace (a lost phone may hold the old token), so "live" there means valid, not `revoked_at is null`. Deno: `_shared/device-credentials.ts` (`newDeviceToken` = 32 random bytes as base64url; `hashDeviceToken` = sha256 of the bytes as hex, null unless exactly 32 bytes of base64url; `verifyDeviceCredential(admin, token, scope)` → user id or null, and throws on a DB error rather than returning null). New function `register-device-credential` (`index.ts` with `withSupabase({ auth: 'user' })`; `request.ts`: POST, 1 KB cap, `{ installationId: uuid }`, `cache-control: no-store`, a 500 logs only the error message; `handler.ts` with the store over the RPC; own `deno.json`/`deno.lock`; no config.toml entry, since it verifies the JWT). Admin portal: a Devices card on the user page with **Revoke device credentials** (`components/revoke-device-credentials.tsx`, like Unblock's dialog, required note; `lib/device-credential-actions.ts`; message from `lib/device-credentials.ts`); Activity label "Revoked device credentials" with summary "2 credentials", and `admin.revoke_device_credentials` in the user activity filter; `AdminRevokeDeviceCredentialsResult` type; a `docs/admin.md` section. **Tests:** pgTAP `67_device_credentials.test.sql` (54: table, RLS, privileges of the table and all four functions; register for 90 days; replace with a 24 h grace and only one live; a near-expiry one never gains time; per installation; bad hash, missing installation, duplicate hash, unknown user; a direct second live insert refused by the index; verify live, grace, out of scope, unknown, expired, revoked, and `last_used_at` set only on verified ones; an added scope; clients can't read the table or call the service functions; sign-out is per user and installation and takes the grace one too; re-register after sign-out; admin: non-admin, blank and null note, revokes live and grace but not expired or signed-out, audit row, 0 still audited; cascade on user deletion). Deno `_shared/device-credentials.test.ts` (8: token encoding incl. base64url characters, hash of known bytes, malformed tokens null without a DB call, RPC args, null, DB error) and `register-device-credential/handler.test.ts` (7: exact RPC args and response, a new token each time never sent to the DB, lower-cased id, 400s, 405/413, 500 without the token in logs, no expiry). - `share-ingest` Edge Function (plan §7, §8): DONE, checked off. **Function** `supabase/functions/share-ingest/` (`index.ts`, `request.ts`, `handler.ts`). `verify_jwt = false` is set in `config.toml` because a device credential isn't a JWT. `withSupabase({ auth: 'none' })` supplies the service-role client. A bearer token with dots is checked as a session through `createSupabaseContext(req, { auth: 'user' })`; any other token goes through `verifyDeviceCredential(…, 'share.ingest')`. Answers, in the order they're checked: 405 · 503 `unavailable` with Retry-After 3600 when `SHARE_IMPORT=off` (before auth) · 401 `unauthorized` · 413 over 2.5 MB (declared or streamed) · 400 `invalid_request` / `invalid_url` (`ShareEnvelopeSchema`) · 400 when `Idempotency-Key` isn't the envelope id (case-insensitive) · 403 `account_mismatch` · 429 `rate_limited` (credentials only). Then the RPC answers: 409 `operation_conflict`, 403 `not_a_member`, 400 `invalid_request` (list not in the household), or 202 `{ id, status }` (`no-store`). `payload_hash` is the sha256 of canonical JSON (keys sorted) of `{ url: canonicalRecipeUrl, householdId, listId (lower-cased), addToList (missing = true), page }`. After a 202 for a job that isn't `done` or `failed`, it POSTs `share-import-worker` with the secret key as `apikey` under `EdgeRuntime.waitUntil`. Errors there are only logged, and that worker is the next task. Each accepted share logs one line (status, via, bytes, JSON-LD count, text bytes), never the page or the token. **Schema:** `ShareEnvelopeSchema` gains optional `v: 2` and `userId` (uuid). **Migration** `20261211120000_share_ingest.sql`: `authenticate_device_credential(hash, scope)` returns `(user_id, credential_id)`, because the per-credential limit and `share_imports.credential_id` need the id. `use_device_credential` is now a wrapper over it, with the same signature and answers. **`verifyDeviceCredential` now returns `{ userId, credentialId }`.** Nothing called it yet; its Deno tests changed to match, and the spec line is updated. The `share_ingest` safety limit (30 a minute, no daily limit) is added to `ai_safety_limits` (the check constraint and `private.safety_limit_keys()`). `check_share_ingest_limit(credential_id)` counts it per credential in `private.share_ingest_usage`. `ingest_share_import(…)` (service role) answers `(outcome, status)`. It checks, in order: an existing id (same user and hash → accepted with its current status, so a resubmit works even after leaving the household; otherwise conflict); the household (as given, else the list's, else the default list's, else the user's first by `joined_at`); membership; and the list belonging to that household (archived or deleted lists are accepted, and the worker falls back). Then it runs `insert … on conflict (id) do nothing`, re-reading the row if it loses a race. **Choices:** an envelope with no household and no list (from an older extension) is resolved as above instead of refused; spec 6.12 now says so. A list that doesn't exist on the server yet (created offline and not pushed) gets a 400. **Admin:** `share_ingest` is added to `admin/lib/safety-limits.ts` ("Share uploads", hint "Per device credential, not AI"), because the drift test requires it. **Tests:**
  - Deno `share-ingest/handler.test.ts` (26): session and credential accepts, with every recorded column; an older envelope; recipe-only; a resubmit after a lost response, including one with a later status; the background upload and the app's submit as one operation; upper-case ids; 409 for each changed part of the payload and for another user; when the worker starts, and a failed start; `account_mismatch` for both kinds of auth; 401 cases; the `Idempotency-Key` check; 10 invalid bodies; `not_a_member` and the list checks; 413 both ways; 429, with sessions not limited; the kill switch; 405; 500 with neither page nor token in the logs; the log line; `canonicalJson`; payload hashes that must match or differ; the store's RPC mapping and errors.
  - `_shared/device-credentials.test.ts`: updated for the new return value.
  - pgTAP `69_share_ingest.test.sql` (42): grants; `authenticate_device_credential` and the wrapper; the limit at 30, refusals not counted, counts per credential, the next minute, a changed or null limit; every ingest outcome and fallback; a bad hash.
  - `60_ai_safety_limits` and `admin/lib/safety-limits.test.ts`: changed to list the new key (the behavior changed).

Added `expo-secure-store` (`yarn expo install`, which also added its config plugin to app.json; v57 docs checked: `accessGroup` is iOS-only and needs the team prefix at runtime, `AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY` exists). New `src/features/auth/device-credential.ts`: `{ token, userId, installationId, expiresAt }` as JSON in Keychain item `share.credential`, service `com.jgp.perfectbite.device-credential` (stored by expo-secure-store as `<service>:no-auth`, noted for the Swift reader), access group `4537GH756L.com.jgp.perfectbite.shared`, `AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY`. The installation id is a v4 uuid in device setting `device.installationId`, added to `KEPT_DEVICE_SETTINGS` so the sign-out wipe keeps it (SQLite rather than Keychain, because Keychain items outlive an uninstall). `ensureDeviceCredential` registers when there's no credential, it belongs to another user or install, or under 30 days are left; one call at a time. `startDeviceCredential` (from `AppDataGate` on sign-in) runs it now and on each foreground. `revokeDeviceCredential` (in `account.signOut`, after `unregisterPush`) calls `revoke_my_device_credential` with a 2 s timeout, best effort, and always deletes the item. `signOutLocally` (account deletion) only deletes it. A sign-out bumps a generation counter, so a registration that answers late isn't stored. iOS only. `keychain-access-groups` `$(AppIdentifierPrefix)com.jgp.perfectbite.shared` added to app.json `ios.entitlements`, to `targets/share-recipe/expo-target.config.js` (taken from the app config) and to the ShareRecipe EAS `appExtensions` entry. `currentListSnapshot` adds `userId`; `ListSnapshotSchema` gets `userId: z.uuid().optional()` and Swift `SiriQueue.ListSnapshot` gets `var userId: String?`. - **App: reconcile shares with the server (share import on the server, plan §7, spec 4.10 step 5).** DONE, checked off. New `src/features/recipes/share-reconcile.ts` replaces the drain on the same triggers (`startShareReconcile` from `app-data.tsx`: launch, sign-in, foreground, reconnect): reads `share/pending/*.json`, keeps the user's envelopes (no `userId` counts as theirs; case-insensitive), deletes unreadable ones (reported); `share_import_status(ids)`; ids the server has → `removeShareFiles(id)` (new in `app-group.ts`: `share/pending/`, `share/upload/`, `share/upload-state/`; `emptyQueues` now empties the upload dirs too); the rest go to `share-ingest` via `functions.invoke` (user's session) with the file's raw text as the body (same bytes as the extension's upload, so the payload hash matches) and `Idempotency-Key: <id>`; 2xx → delete files; 400/409/413/403 `not_a_member` → delete and report to Sentry (judgment call: the server will never take them, and leaving them makes a share wait forever); 403 `account_mismatch` → keep, go on; offline → stop, `offline` on the card; 401/429/5xx/503 → stop, retry next time. Another user's envelopes are never submitted or deleted. Then it reads `my_share_imports` (`neq status done`, by `created_at`) and publishes the card. Runs coalesce (a call during a run runs once more after). The card also refreshes (no submits) after every pull (sync activity pulling true→false, so Realtime pulls count), on a `recipe_import` push arriving (`Notifications.addNotificationReceivedListener` in `startShareReconcile`), and after Try again. `share-queue-status.ts` is now `{ queued: QueuedShare[] (onServer flag), failed: FailedShareImport[], offline }`; `shareFailureReason(code, resetsAt)` mirrors send-notifications' wording (new strings `reasons.notAMember`, `reasons.somethingWrong`, `waitBackground` "We'll prepare them in the background.", en + es); removed the drain-only strings (`progress`, `tried*`, `waitBusy/Idle/Failed`) and the `importing` tone. `imports-card.tsx`: failed shares with Try again (`retryShareImport(id)` → `retry_share_import` with the same id) and no Dismiss (Q17 open); waiting shares with Remove only while the server doesn't have them, a spinner after. - Share extension: background upload and completion (plan §3): `ShareQueue.Envelope` gains `v` (2) and `userId` (from the list snapshot; `ShareEnvelopeSchema` already had both as optional). `ShareQueue.enqueue` returns the bytes written, and `ShareModel.save` then calls `ShareUpload.start`. It reads the device credential from the shared Keychain the way expo-secure-store writes it (`ShareCredential`). Only when the credential is for the same `userId` (ignoring case) and hasn't expired, it writes `share/upload/<id>.json` (the envelope bytes, the same payload the app's reconcile submits) and `share/upload-state/<id>.json` (`{ startedAt, attempts: 1 }`), then starts a `URLSessionUploadTask` from that file. The task runs on the background session `com.jgp.perfectbite.share.upload` (`sharedContainerIdentifier` the App Group), with `Authorization: Bearer` and `Idempotency-Key`, and posts to `ShareIngestURL` from the extension's Info.plist. The Info.plist value is `$(SHARE_INGEST_URL)`. @bacons/apple-targets 5 has no option for Info.plist keys or build settings, so `targets/share-recipe/expo-target.config.js` registers an Xcode-project mod (`withXcodeProjectBeta`). Registered before apple-targets adds the target, it runs after it, and sets that build setting on the ShareRecipe target to `EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` + `/functions/v1/share-ingest` (the env loaded with the app config). With no URL it removes the setting, and the extension skips the upload. Added `NSAllowsLocalNetworking` to the extension's ATS so it can reach local Supabase over LAN http. The sheet says "Saved. We'll prepare it in the background." in both switch states: `ShareChoice.savedMessage` is now a constant, and `shareSavedMessage(addToList)` became `SHARE_SAVED_MESSAGE` in save-choice.ts (its test changed because the behavior did). New local Expo module `modules/share-upload` (apple only, `appDelegateSubscribers: ["ShareUploadAppDelegate"]`). For our session identifier it recreates the background session once. On a 2xx its delegate deletes `share/upload/<id>.json` and sets `accepted`/`acceptedAt` in the state file (only if that file still exists); anything else leaves both. It calls the completion handler from `urlSessionDidFinishEvents`; for other identifiers it calls the handler at once. Only reconciliation deletes the envelope. **Tests:** `share.test.ts`: new `share-envelope.v2.json` fixture as Swift writes it, v2 without userId, a bad `v`/`userId` rejected, older fixtures have neither. `share-extension.test.ts`, new contract tests: on both Swift sides the session id, App Group and paths match `app-group.ts`; the Keychain names match `device-credential.ts`; the upload starts after the atomic write, with the headers and from the body file; the state fields; the 2xx rule on both sides and no envelope deletion; the module config and subscriber shape; the Info.plist key; `shareIngestUrl` cases; `setShareIngestUrl` touches only ShareRecipe; the registered mod sets the URL from the env. `save-choice.test.ts` updated. There's no Swift unit-test target, and the Swift wasn't compiled here (running `xcrun swiftc` needed approval), so the build check went to manual-checks.md. **Checks:** jest before 212 / 5637 passed, after 212 / 5652 passed, 2 skipped; lint and tsc clean. Local migration `0031_failed_photo_imports` (drizzle-kit table rebuild; its generated INSERT selected the new columns from the old table, so I fixed it to put `'link'` and NULL there): `failed_imports.kind` (`link` | `photo`, default `link`, existing rows `link`), `pages` (JSON file names, null for links), `url` nullable; rows kept. New `src/features/recipes/failed-photo-pages.ts` (expo-file-system v57 `File.moveSync` / `Directory.delete`, checked in the installed typings): `recordFailedPhotoImport` moves each sent page into `<Paths.document>/failed-imports/<id>/<n>.jpg` (n from 1, in order) and inserts the `photo` row with `recordedTarget`; if a page can't be moved it records nothing and deletes what moved (judgment call: a row missing a page can't be retried properly). `updateFailedPhotoImport(id, { pages, householdId, listId })` takes the pages as URIs (the row's own, from `failedImportPageUris`, plus new ones, which move in under the next unused number), deletes the removed pages' files and saves the target; false for no pages, a link row, an unknown row or a page that can't be moved. `pruneFailedPhotoImports` (on launch, in `AppDataGate`'s launch steps) deletes photo rows 30 days old or more, or whose files are missing, with their folders, plus folders no row owns. Link rows aren't pruned (the spec ties the 30 days to the pages). `importRecipeFromPhotos` records on every failure except no photos (every other failure is notified) and returns `failedImportId` on the error, for the UI task's notification → review sheet. A `retryOf` input (the Try again) records nothing and returns that id. `retryFailedImport` sends a photo row's kept pages through the photo path with the same target fallbacks; on success the pages go up via `uploadRecipePages` (keepPages), then the row and folder are removed; on failure the row and files stay with the new reason. A photo row whose files are gone is deleted quietly (null). `dismissFailedImport` and `wipeLocalData` (`deleteAllFailedPages`) delete the folder too. `failedImportName` gives "From a photo" for a row without a url (new string `recipes.imports.fromAPhoto`, en + es "De una foto"), and `failedImportReason(reason, now, kind)` uses the photo notification's wording for `kind: 'photo'`. `src/test/fake-file-system.ts` gained `uri`, `file://` paths, `moveSync`, `base64`, `arrayBuffer`, `Directory.delete`/`name` and `Paths.document` (`setDocumentRoot`). **Tests:** new `src/db/__tests__/failed-photo-imports-migration.test.ts` (4: existing links kept as links with every field, column shapes and default, a photo row with no url). `failed-imports.test.ts` (+17): photo copy; recording with real files (moved out of the cache, contents and order kept, row shape, page URIs); a missing cache page or no pages records nothing; importRecipeFromPhotos records on failure. - Recipe collections, browsing (boards A and B). Baseline jest before changes: 215 suites, 5786 passed, 2 skipped. - Recipe collections, adding many at once (board D). Baseline jest before changes: 216 suites, 5823 passed, 2 skipped. **Recipes tab Select mode** (`src/app/(tabs)/recipes/index.tsx`): **Select** (`checkmark.circle`) in the card's long-press menu (rows, large tiles and shelf tiles; after Add to collection…, before Rename) enters it with that recipe ticked. The green header swaps the title row for Cancel and Select All (Deselect All once every shown one is ticked, as on the collection screen) over "N selected" / "Tap recipes to select them". The sort button, +, imports card, shelves and All recipes heading give way. Rows and tiles tick (`RecipeRow`/`RecipeTile` `select`; tiles got a tick over the photo's corner and a `primary` outline). Ticks are a set of ids that outlasts search and filters (the count includes hidden ones). Select All takes every recipe shown in the selection's household (the first household shown while nothing's ticked). Ticking another household's recipe shows the toast "Pick recipes from one household at a time." and doesn't tick it. The bottom bar's **Add to collection** pushes the new form-sheet route `src/app/add-to-collection.tsx` (`ids` comma-joined). The Add to collection sheet moved into `src/features/recipes/add-to-collection-sheet.tsx`, shared by `recipe/[id]/collections.tsx` (one recipe, behaviour unchanged) and the new route. The new route shows "4 recipes" under the title and ticks a collection only when every recipe is in it (`readCollectionsHoldingAll`). Done runs `applyCollectionTicksToAll`: it adds all of them to newly ticked collections and removes all of them from collections unticked since the sheet opened. A collection holding only some of them starts unticked, so leaving it unticked changes nothing (my reading of "removes all from unticked ones"). Done hands `collection-ticks-result.ts` to the tab, which leaves Select mode and shows `collectionTicksToast` ("Added 4 recipes to Mom's cookbook", "… to 2 collections", or "Removed … from …"; no toast when nothing changed). **Add recipes sheet**: new form-sheet route `src/app/collection/[id]/add-recipes.tsx`; the collection screen's Add recipes buttons now open it (TODO removed). It lists the household's recipes A–Z under a search field. A tap ticks a recipe; ones already in the collection show ticked, dimmed and disabled, "Already in Mom's cookbook". Once something's ticked, a bar shows **Add 3 to Mom's cookbook** (`addRecipesToCollection`, then back). VoiceOver: Select-mode rows and tiles (and the collection screen's, which share `RecipeRow`) are buttons with `selected` state, labelled "Pot roast, selected" when ticked (`selectLabel`). en and es strings under `recipes.collections`. Both routes registered in `_layout.tsx`; `.expo/types/router.d.ts` (generated, untracked) edited by hand for them, as before. **Tests added:** `collections-select.test.tsx` (25: entering Select mode from the menu with header/title/bar/VoiceOver label, Cancel, tick/untick and the empty title with the bar off, Select All / Deselect All after a filter with a hidden tick kept, ticks kept through searches, the mixed-household refusal toast and Select All staying in one household, large-photo tiles, the bar's push, Done's result leaving Select mode with and without a toast; the multi-recipe sheet's subtitle and all-or-nothing ticks, Done adding with the result and toast, unticking removing all while a partial collection is untouched, Cancel, missing recipes; `readCollectionsHoldingAll`, `applyCollectionTicksToAll`, `collectionTicksToast` incl. Spanish; the Add recipes sheet: opened from the collection screen, rows A–Z with the disabled already-in ones and the other household's left out, ticking and adding, the bar hiding, search with ticks kept and no match, an empty household, a recipe added elsewhere showing as already in). **Existing tests changed because the behavior changed:** the card long-press menu title lists in `recipe-import-screens`, `recipes-tab-photo-size`, `recipes-tab-saved` and `recipes-tab-shelves` now include Select. `collections-browse.test.tsx`'s Select-mode helpers now read `accessibilityState.selected` and strip the ", selected" label suffix (before, the rows were checkboxes with `checked` and a bare name). **Checks:** jest 217 suites, 5848 passed, 2 skipped; lint and tsc clean. - Recipe collections, filtering (board E). Baseline jest before changes: 217 suites, 5848 passed, 2 skipped. `RecipeFilters` gains `collections` (picked collection names) and `FilterFacetKey` gains `'collections'`. Names compare by `collectionNameKey` (new in `recipe-filters.ts`, ignoring case and accents; `collections.ts` now uses it for its duplicate check), so one pick matches every collection with that name in any household. Matching is OR within the group and AND with the other facets and the search. Picks count in `activeFilterCount` (the Filters button and Show N recipes), and their chips come after Saved's and before Meal's, in pick order. `availableFilterFacets` takes a 4th `collections` argument (`CollectionsByRecipe`, recipe id → collection names) and adds the **Collections** group right after Saved: one pill per name that a collection holding one of the given recipes has, A–Z, with the same name shown once (the first spelling under a full-sensitivity collator), plus any picked name so it can be turned off. With no `collections` given there's no group, so the collection screen's sheet leaves it out. `matchesRecipeFilters`/`filterRecipes` take the same 4th argument. `collections.ts` adds `collectionsByRecipe`, `readCollectionsByRecipe`/`useCollectionsByRecipe` (live memberships in live collections, every household) and `readCollectionNames`. `recipe-filters.ts` adds `withoutGoneCollections`, and `recipe-filter-store.ts` adds `dropGoneCollectionFilters`, which the Recipes tab runs whenever the collections change. It reads the names straight from the database, because the live query starts empty and would otherwise wipe the picks on mount. A deleted collection drops out of the picks, and so does a renamed one, since picks are names. `RecipeFilterPanel` takes an optional `collections` prop. The Recipes tab, its filter sheet and the plan slot sheet pass it (the slot sheet reads once, like its favorites, so it shows only the household's collections). `slotSheetRecipes` takes `collections` in its options. The list's Add to recipe sheet doesn't get the group, because the task didn't name it. Untagged is unchanged: being in a collection doesn't make a recipe tagged. en/es string `recipes.filters.collections` ("Collections" / "Colecciones"). **Tests added:** `recipe-filters.test.ts` (14: name key, OR within, AND across, same name in two households with either spelling, no data or no membership, group order after Saved and first without Saved values, none without data, only collections with a recipe, a picked name staying, toggling by another spelling and leaving Untagged, the count, chips and their removal, `withoutGoneCollections`, the Spanish group name); `collections-browse.test.tsx` (6: the tab's sheet shows the group after Saved A–Z without the empty collection; picks narrow the count and the tab, with the Filters count and chips; the group hidden with no collection holding a recipe, while a picked one stays; two households with the same name shown once and matching both; a deleted collection dropping out of the picks; the collection's own sheet without the group); `collections.test.ts` (2: `readCollectionsByRecipe`/`readCollectionNames` leaving out deleted collections and memberships, `collectionsByRecipe`); `slot.test.tsx` (2: the slot panel's Collections group with only the household's collections that have recipes, a pick narrowing with chips, and no group without collections). No existing tests changed. **Checks:** jest 217 suites, 5872 passed, 2 skipped; lint and tsc clean. No supabase/ changes. No real AI calls.
